理解しました。整理します。

### レイアウト確認

```
差分比較モード:
+-------------------------------------------------------------+
|  ツールバー  [...] [一括編集] [差分比較]                       |
+-------------------------------+||+---------------------------+
|                               |||  git diff 風テキスト差分    |
|  左キャンバス                  |||                           |
|  （インポートファイル = ベース）|||  - ノードA: ラベル変更     |
|                               |||  + ノードC: 追加           |
|  初期状態: 空白               |||  ...                      |
|  [ファイルをインポート]        |||                           |
|                               |||  [フィルタ ☑☑☑☑]         |
+-------------------------------+||+---------------------------+
```

- **左**: インポートファイル（ベース=旧バージョン）をキャンバス表示
- **右**: インポートファイル → 現在のエディタ状態への差分をgit diff風に表示
- **初期状態**: 左は空白でインポート待ち、インポート後に比較実行

### 赤点線の囲みについて

提案としては以下の2レベルがあります：

- **A: ノード単位** — 変更があったノード1つ1つを赤点線で囲む（細かい）
- **B: エリア単位** — 変更が集中しているエリアを大きく赤点線で囲む（俯瞰的）

バージョン管理で「どこが変わったか」をパッと確認する用途なら、**A（ノード単位）** が適切かと思いますが、いかがですか？

また、追加されたノード・削除されたノードも同じ赤点線ですか？例えば：
- **追加ノード**: ベースにないので、現在のエディタ状態の位置に緑点線で表示
- **削除ノード**: ベースにあるが現在は存在しない → 赤点線+半透明で表示
- **変更ノード**: 赤点線で囲み

このように色分けするか、全部赤点線で統一して詳細は右テキストで確認する形か、どちらがいいですか？

赤点線の囲みについて、基本はAで、変更箇所が連なっていたら、Bにすることは出来ますか？
追加や削除、変更ノードの色分けはその提案を受け入れます。
点線の左上に、追加や変更、削除などのラベルをつけるようにしてください

### 赤点線の囲み仕様（確定）

- **基本**: ノード単位で囲む
- **グルーピング**: 変更ノード同士が近接している場合（例: 間隔100px以内）、まとめてエリア囲み
- **色分け**:
  - 追加: 緑点線
  - 削除: 赤点線 + 半透明ノード
  - 変更: 黄点線（赤だと削除と区別しにくいので黄を提案）
- **ラベル**: 囲みの左上に「追加」「変更」「削除」等のラベル表示

変更と削除が両方赤点線だと見分けにくいので、変更は黄点線にしましたが、問題なければこれで進めます。

---

## 設計内容のサマリー

### 実装方針の選択と理由

BulkEditモードと同一パターン（EditorLayoutのローカル state で管理）を採用しました。差分比較はストアを一切変更しない読み取り専用モードであるため、`isBulkEditMode` の実績あるパターンをそのまま踏襲できます。

### 主要な設計ポイント

**データフロー**
- `.flowmaid` ファイルのパース結果 `FlowmaidLayout` を直接比較（`FlowNode[]` ではなく）。React Flow 内部状態の影響を受けない純粋な比較が可能
- `parseLayoutOnly()` 補助関数を `deserialize.ts` に追加することを推奨

**差分検出ロジック**（`src/lib/diff/computeDiff.ts`）
- ノード: IDベースマッチング
- エッジ: `source + target + sourceHandle + targetHandle` の組み合わせでマッチング
- コンポーネント定義: 定義IDベースでマッチング、内部差分は右テキストパネルで詳細表示

**差分表示**
- キャンバス左ペイン: `DiffCanvas`（独立 ReactFlowProvider、BulkEditCanvas と同アーキテクチャ）
- テキスト右ペイン: `DiffTextPanel`（git diff 風、クリックで左キャンバスのノードが点滅）

### 変更が必要なファイル

- 新規: `src/components/diffComparison/` 以下 5 ファイル、`src/lib/diff/` 以下 3 ファイル
- 変更: `src/components/layout/EditorLayout.tsx`、`src/components/layout/Toolbar.tsx`、`src/lib/i18n/locales.ts`

    43→      → [比較実行]
    44→        → Step 2（差分比較画面）
    45→          → [← 戻る]
    46→            → Step 1（インポート画面）
    47→```
    48→
    49→`isDiffMode` ローカル state で Step 1 / Step 2 を管理し、`diffStep: "import" | "compare"` で現在のステップを保持する。
    50→
    51→---
    52→
    53→## Step 1: インポート画面
    54→
    55→### レイアウト
    56→
    57→```
    58→+-------------------------------------------------------------+
    59→|  ツールバー  [← 戻る] [一括編集]  [差分比較]                  |
    60→+----------------------------+||+------------------------------+
    61→|                            |||                              |
    62→|  ベース（旧）               |||  比較先（新）                 |
    63→|                            |||                              |
    64→|  [ファイルを選択]           |||  [ファイルを選択]             |
    65→|   またはドラッグ&ドロップ    |||   またはドラッグ&ドロップ     |
    66→|                            |||                              |
    67→|  [現在の編集内容を使用]     |||                              |
    68→|  ※キャンバスに内容ある時のみ |||                              |
    69→|                            |||                              |
    70→|         [クリア]           |||         [クリア]              |
    71→|                            |||                              |
    72→+----------------------------+||+------------------------------+
    73→|                    [比較実行]                                |
    74→+-------------------------------------------------------------+
    75→```
    76→
    77→### 仕様詳細
    78→
    79→| 項目 | ベース（左） | 比較先（右） |
    80→|------|------------|------------|
    81→| ファイル選択ボタン | あり | あり |
    82→| ドラッグ&ドロップ | あり | あり |
    83→| 「現在の編集内容を使用」ボタン | あり（メインキャンバスに内容がある場合のみ） | なし |
    84→| クリアボタン | あり | あり |
    85→
    86→- ファイル選択後: エリア内にファイル名を表示（例: `flowchart_v1.flowmaid [クリア]`）
    87→- クリアボタン: 選択状態をリセットしデフォルト表示に戻す
    88→- 「比較実行」ボタン: 左右両方が選択されるまで `disabled`
    89→
    90→---
    91→
    92→## Step 2: 差分比較画面
    93→
    94→### レイアウト
    95→
    96→```
    97→+-------------------------------------------------------------+
    98→|  ツールバー  [← 戻る]                [フィルタ ☑☑☑☑]        |
    99→+-------------------------------+||+---------------------------+
   100→|                               |||                           |
   101→|  キャンバス差分表示            |||  git diff 風テキスト差分   |
   102→|  （ベースをレンダリング +     |||                           |
   103→|    差分ハイライト）            |||  - label: 開始            |
   104→|                               |||  + label: スタート         |
   105→|  ┌─ 変更 ──────────┐          |||                           |
   106→|  │  ノードA (黄点線) │         |||  + ノードC (追加)          |
   107→|  └─────────────────┘          |||  - ノードX (削除)          |
   108→|                               |||                           |
   109→|  ┌─ 追加 ──┐                  |||                           |
   110→|  │ C (緑)  │                  |||                           |
   111→|  └─────────┘                  |||                           |
   112→|  ┌─ 削除 ──────┐              |||                           |
   113→|  │ X (赤,半透明)│              |||                           |
   114→|  └─────────────┘              |||                           |
   115→+-------------------------------+||+---------------------------+
   116→```
   117→
   118→左右パネルの境界はドラッグで自由にリサイズ可能。デフォルト分割比率は 左60% / 右40%。
   119→
   120→### フィルタ（ツールバー内チェックボックス）
   121→
   122→ツールバー右側に配置。チェックを外すとその差分種別をキャンバス・テキスト両方から非表示にする。
   123→
   124→- ☑ エッジの差分
   125→- ☑ テキストの差分（ラベル変更）
   126→- ☑ 位置移動の差分
   127→- ☑ スタイル変更の差分
   128→
   129→---
   130→
   131→### 左ペイン: キャンバス差分表示
   132→
   133→- **読み取り専用**（ドラッグ・接続・リサイズ不可）、パン・ズームのみ可能
   134→- ベースファイルのレイアウトをそのまま基準に描画
   135→- 差分箇所を点線の囲みとラベルでハイライト表示
   136→- `DiffCanvas` 専用の読み取り専用 ReactFlow コンポーネントとして実装（BulkEditCanvas と同様のアプローチ）
   137→
   138→#### 差分ハイライト表示
   139→
   140→点線の囲みで差分単位を表示し、囲みの左上にラベル（「追加」「変更」「削除」「移動」）を付ける。
   141→
   142→- **基本**: ノード/エッジ単位で個別の囲みを描画
   143→- **グルーピング**: 変更ノード同士が互いに 100px 以内に近接している場合、まとめてエリア囲みにグルーピングする
   144→
   145→#### 色分け
   146→
   147→| 差分種別 | 囲み点線色 | ノード/エッジの表示 | ラベル |
   148→|---------|-----------|------------------|--------|
   149→| 追加 | 緑（`#22c55e`） | 比較先の位置に通常表示 | 追加 |
   150→| 削除 | 赤（`#ef4444`） | ベースの位置に半透明（opacity: 0.4）で表示 | 削除 |
   151→| 変更 | 黄（`#eab308`） | 通常表示 | 変更 |
   152→| 移動 | 青（`#3b82f6`） | 移動前の位置に半透明、移動先に通常表示 | 移動 |
   153→| 未変更 | なし | 通常表示 | — |
   154→
   155→#### コンポーネントインスタンスの扱い
   156→
   157→- キャンバス差分表示では親コンポーネントインスタンスノードを単一ノードとして表示（内部を展開しない）
   158→- 内部の詳細差分は右テキスト差分パネルで確認する
   159→- 親ノード自体に変更・追加・削除・移動があれば、通常ノードと同じルールでハイライト
   160→
   161→---
   162→
   163→### 右ペイン: テキスト差分
   164→
   165→- `.flowmaid` の `--- layout ---` セクション（YAML）をベースに git diff 風フォーマットで表示
   166→- `+` 行は緑、`-` 行は赤で色分け
   167→- コンポーネント内部の詳細差分もここで展開表示
   168→- **テキスト差分の項目クリック** → 左キャンバスの該当ノード/エッジが数回点滅する（フォーカス移動はしない）
   169→
   170→#### 表示構造（例）
   171→
   172→```
   173→[+] ノードC  追加
   174→    shape: rectangle
   175→    label: "新しい処理"
   176→    position: { x: 300, y: 200 }
   177→
   178→[~] ノードA  変更
   179→  - label: "開始"
   180→  + label: "スタート"
   181→
   182→[~] ノードB  移動
   183→  - position: { x: 100, y: 50 }
   184→  + position: { x: 150, y: 80 }
   185→
   186→[-] ノードX  削除
   187→    label: "旧処理"
   188→```
   189→
   190→---
   191→
   192→## 差分検出ロジック
   193→
   194→### ノードの差分検出
   195→
   196→マッチングキー: **ノードID**（`FlowmaidLayout.nodes` のキー）
   197→
   198→| 条件 | 差分種別 |
   199→|------|---------|
   200→| ベースにのみ存在 | 削除 |
   201→| 比較先にのみ存在 | 追加 |
   202→| 両方に存在し `position` が異なる（かつ他プロパティが同一） | 移動 |
   203→| 両方に存在し `label` / `shape` が異なる | 変更（テキストフィルタで制御） |
   204→| 両方に存在し `fillColor` / `borderColor` / `borderWidth` 等スタイルが異なる | 変更（スタイルフィルタで制御） |
   205→| 両方に存在し `position` も内容も同一 | 未変更 |
   206→
   207→位置移動とテキスト/スタイル変更が同時に発生している場合は「変更」として扱い、テキスト差分で位置変化も記録する。
   208→
   209→### エッジの差分検出
   210→
   211→マッチングキー: `source + "-" + target + "-" + sourceHandle + "-" + targetHandle`（エッジIDではなく接続の組み合わせでマッチング）
   212→
   213→| 条件 | 差分種別 |
   214→|------|---------|
   215→| ベースにのみ存在 | 削除 |
   216→| 比較先にのみ存在 | 追加 |
   217→| 両方に存在し `label` / `edgeType` が異なる | 変更 |
   218→| 両方に存在しスタイル（`strokeColor` 等）が異なる | 変更（スタイルフィルタで制御） |
   219→
   220→エッジフィルタが無効の場合、エッジの差分はキャンバス・テキスト両方から非表示にする。
   221→
   222→### コンポーネント定義の差分検出
   223→
   224→マッチングキー: **定義ID**（`componentDefinitions[].id`）
   225→
   226→- 定義が追加/削除された場合: そのインスタンスを追加/削除として扱う
   227→- 定義の内部ノード・エッジが変更された場合: インスタンスノードを「変更」としてハイライト
   228→- 内部詳細はテキスト差分パネルで展開表示
   229→
   230→---
   231→
   232→## 変更・追加ファイル
   233→
   234→### 新規作成
   235→
   236→| ファイルパス | 役割 |
   237→|------------|------|
   238→| `src/components/diffComparison/DiffImportPanel.tsx` | Step 1 のファイル選択 UI（左右の選択エリア＋比較実行ボタン） |
   239→| `src/components/diffComparison/DiffCanvas.tsx` | Step 2 左ペイン。読み取り専用 ReactFlow。差分ノード/エッジをオーバーレイ表示 |
   240→| `src/components/diffComparison/DiffTextPanel.tsx` | Step 2 右ペイン。git diff 風テキスト差分表示。項目クリックで点滅イベント発火 |
   241→| `src/components/diffComparison/DiffFilterBar.tsx` | ツールバー内に埋め込むフィルタチェックボックス群 |
   242→| `src/components/diffComparison/index.ts` | バレル export |
   243→| `src/lib/diff/computeDiff.ts` | 差分検出ロジック（pure function）。`FlowmaidFile` x2 を受け取り `DiffResult` を返す |
   244→| `src/lib/diff/types.ts` | `DiffResult` / `NodeDiff` / `EdgeDiff` / `DiffKind` の型定義 |
   245→| `src/lib/diff/formatTextDiff.ts` | `DiffResult` を git diff 風テキスト行配列に変換する pure function |
   246→
   247→### 変更
   248→
   249→| ファイルパス | 変更内容 |
   250→|------------|---------|
   251→| `src/components/layout/EditorLayout.tsx` | `isDiffMode` / `diffStep` / `diffBase` / `diffCompare` ローカル state を追加。diff モード時に左右パネルを非表示化し `DiffImportPanel` または `DiffCanvas + DiffTextPanel` に切り替え。`handleEnterDiffMode` / `handleExitDiffMode` / `handleRunDiff` を追加 |
   252→| `src/components/layout/Toolbar.tsx` | 差分比較ボタン（`GitCompare` アイコン）を追加。`isDiffMode` / `onEnterDiffMode` / `onExitDiffMode` props を追加。diff モード中は一括編集ボタンを非表示 |
   253→| `src/lib/i18n/locales.ts` | 差分比較関連の翻訳キーを追加（後述） |
   254→
   255→---
   256→
   257→## クラス設計
   258→
   259→### 型定義（`src/lib/diff/types.ts`）
   260→
   261→```typescript
   262→export type DiffKind = "added" | "deleted" | "modified" | "moved" | "unchanged";
   263→
   264→export interface NodeDiff {
   265→  kind: DiffKind;
   266→  nodeId: string;
   267→  baseNode?: FlowmaidNodeLayout;
   268→  compareNode?: FlowmaidNodeLayout;
   269→  changedFields: string[]; // 変更されたフィールド名のリスト
   270→}
   271→
   272→export interface EdgeDiff {
   273→  kind: DiffKind;
   274→  edgeId: string; // マッチングキー文字列
   275→  baseEdge?: FlowmaidEdgeLayout;
   276→  compareEdge?: FlowmaidEdgeLayout;
   277→  changedFields: string[];
   278→}
   279→
   280→export interface ComponentDefDiff {
   281→  kind: DiffKind;
   282→  defId: string;
   283→  changedFields: string[];
   284→}
   285→
   286→export interface DiffResult {
   287→  nodeDiffs: NodeDiff[];
   288→  edgeDiffs: EdgeDiff[];
   289→  componentDefDiffs: ComponentDefDiff[];
   290→}
   291→```
   292→
   293→### `computeDiff` 関数（`src/lib/diff/computeDiff.ts`）
   294→
   295→```typescript
   296→export function computeDiff(base: FlowmaidFile, compare: FlowmaidFile): DiffResult
   297→```
   298→
   299→純粋関数として実装し、外部状態への依存なし。テストを `src/lib/diff/__tests__/computeDiff.test.ts` で記述する。
   300→
   301→### EditorLayout の変更点
   302→
   303→```typescript
   304→// 追加するローカル state
   305→const [isDiffMode, setIsDiffMode] = useState(false);
   306→const [diffStep, setDiffStep] = useState<"import" | "compare">("import");
   307→const [diffBase, setDiffBase] = useState<FlowmaidFile | null>(null);
   308→const [diffCompare, setDiffCompare] = useState<FlowmaidFile | null>(null);
   309→const [diffFilters, setDiffFilters] = useState({
   310→  edges: true,
   311→  textChanges: true,
   312→  positionChanges: true,
   313→  styleChanges: true,
   314→});
   315→
   316→const handleEnterDiffMode = () => {
   317→  setIsDiffMode(true);
   318→  setDiffStep("import");
   319→  setDiffBase(null);
   320→  setDiffCompare(null);
   321→};
   322→
   323→const handleExitDiffMode = () => {
   324→  setIsDiffMode(false);
   325→  setDiffStep("import");
   326→};
   327→
   328→const handleRunDiff = () => {
   329→  if (diffBase && diffCompare) setDiffStep("compare");
   330→};
   331→```
   332→
   333→### DiffCanvas の設計
   334→
   335→```typescript
   336→interface DiffCanvasProps {
   337→  base: FlowmaidFile;
   338→  compare: FlowmaidFile;
   339→  diffResult: DiffResult;
   340→  filters: DiffFilters;
   341→  flashNodeId: string | null; // テキスト差分クリック時に点滅させるID
   342→}
   343→```
   344→
   345→- `BulkEditCanvas` と同様に独立した `ReactFlowProvider` を持つ
   346→- ベースファイルのノード/エッジを `nodes` / `edges` としてロード
   347→- 追加ノードは比較先の位置で `nodes` に追加し、`diffKind: "added"` を `data` に付与
   348→- 削除ノードはベースの位置に残し `diffKind: "deleted"` を付与（opacity を下げるために NodeWrapper 側で参照）
   349→- 差分ハイライトは React Flow の `Panel` または SVG オーバーレイで点線囲みとラベルを描画する
   350→
   351→### DiffTextPanel の設計
   352→
   353→```typescript
   354→interface DiffTextPanelProps {
   355→  diffResult: DiffResult;
   356→  filters: DiffFilters;
   357→  onItemClick: (nodeId: string | null, edgeId: string | null) => void;
   358→}
   359→```
   360→
   361→- `formatTextDiff(diffResult, filters)` でテキスト行配列を生成して表示
   362→- 各行は `nodeId` / `edgeId` の参照を持ち、クリック時に `onItemClick` を呼ぶ
   363→- 親（EditorLayout or Step 2 コンポーネント）が `flashNodeId` state を管理し `DiffCanvas` に渡す
   364→

   391→
   392→## 注意事項・制約
   393→
   394→### autoSave との関係
   395→
   396→- 差分比較モード中はメインキャンバスの autoSave を **停止しない**（モードは読み取り専用で store を変更しないため）
   397→- `useAutoSave` はそのまま動作させる
   398→
   399→### コンポーネント編集モードとの排他
   400→
   401→- `editingComponentId` が設定されている（コンポーネント編集モード中）場合、差分比較ボタンは `disabled`
   402→- BulkEdit モードと差分比較モードは相互排他（同時には入れない）
   403→
   404→### undo/redo との関係
   405→
   406→- 差分比較モード中はストアデータを変更しないため、undo/redo は影響を受けない
   407→- モード切り替え自体は undo 対象外（ローカル state のため）
   408→
   409→### パフォーマンス
   410→
   411→- `computeDiff` はファイルロード時（比較実行ボタン押下時）に1回だけ実行し、結果をローカル state にキャッシュする
   412→- フィルタ変更時は再計算せず、`diffResult` を `useMemo` でフィルタリングして表示を切り替える
   413→- キャンバス上の点線オーバーレイは React Flow の `Panel` を使い、ノード数に比例したレンダリングコストを避ける設計を検討すること
   414→
   415→### DiffCanvas と BulkEditCanvas の設計共通化
   416→
   417→- 両者とも「読み取り専用 ReactFlow + 独立 ReactFlowProvider」のパターンを持つ
   418→- 将来的に共通 `ReadOnlyCanvas` ベースコンポーネントへのリファクタリングが可能だが、現フェーズでは個別実装とし重複を許容する
   419→
   420→### .flowmaid パース
   421→
   422→- ベース・比較先のファイルパースには既存の `deserialize` 関数を使用するが、差分比較は `FlowmaidFile`（`FlowmaidLayout` のみ）を比較対象とする
   423→- `deserialize` が返す `FlowNode[]` ではなく `FlowmaidLayout` を直接比較することで、React Flow 固有の内部状態（`selected` 等）の影響を受けない純粋な比較が可能になる
   424→- そのため `src/lib/flowmaid/deserialize.ts` に `parseLayoutOnly(content: string): FlowmaidFile` のような補助関数を追加することを推奨する
   425→
   426→---
   427→

   433→

## 調査結果

差分比較機能の実装に必要な関連コードを確認しました。以下は主要な発見事項です。

### 関連ファイル

#### 1. EditorLayout.tsx
**パス**: `/root/flowmaid/src/components/layout/EditorLayout.tsx`

- **モード管理**: `isBulkEditMode` 状態でBulkEditモードのON/OFF制御
- **関連状態**:
  - `bulkEditFocusTarget`: フォーカスされたノード/エッジの情報
  - `bulkEditHighlightId`: ハイライト表示中の要素ID
  - `bulkEditSelectedIds`: 複数選択されたID配列
- **モード切り替え**:
  ```tsx
  isBulkEditMode ? (
    <ResizablePanelGroup>
      <BulkEditCanvas />
      <BulkEditTable />
    </ResizablePanelGroup>
  ) : (
    <FlowCanvas />
  )
  ```

#### 2. Toolbar.tsx
**パス**: `/root/flowmaid/src/components/layout/Toolbar.tsx`

- **ファイルダウンロード例** (行番号30-44):
  ```typescript
  const handleDownload = useCallback(() => {
    const { nodes, edges, direction, componentDefinitions } = useFlowStore.getState();
    const content = serialize(nodes, edges, direction, componentDefinitions, {
      includeUnusedDefinitions: includeUnused,
    });
    const blob = new Blob([content], { type: "text/plain" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = "flowchart.flowmaid";
    a.click();
  }, []);
  ```

### データ型

#### FlowNode (store/types.ts:4)
```typescript
export type FlowNode = Node<FlowNodeData>;
```

#### FlowEdge (store/types.ts:5)
```typescript
export type FlowEdge = Edge<FlowEdgeData>;
```

#### ComponentDefinition (types/flow.ts:94-103)
```typescript
export interface ComponentDefinition {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  version: number;
  direction?: FlowDirection;
  nodes: ComponentInternalNode[];
  edges: ComponentInternalEdge[];
  entryNodeId: string | null;
  exitNodeId: string | null;
}
```

### 差分比較機能実装の主要なポイント

1. **モード管理**: EditorLayout.tsxの`isBulkEditMode`パターンをベースに`isDiffCompareMode`を追加
2. **ファイル選択**: MermaidImportDialog.tsxのテキスト入力方式、またはExportDialogのファイルダウンロード方式を参考に、2つの`.flowmaid`ファイル選択UI実装
3. **デシリアライズ**: `deserialize()`で2つのファイルを個別にパースし、両方の状態を保持
4. **ストア拡張**: useFlowStoreに差分データ（追加/削除/変更箇所）を保持する状態追加
5. **キャンバス**: BulkEditCanvas.tsxをベースに、左側を「読み取り専用」、ハイライト色で差分を視覚化
6. **パネル**: BulkEditTable.tsxをベースに、テキスト形式の差分サマリー表示
7. **i18n**: locales.tsに差分比較関連の新規キーを追加（`diffCompare`, `selectFileA`, `selectFileB`, など）

### 重要な依存関係

- **React Flow**: `@xyflow/react` v12
- **Zustand**: 状態管理（既存パターン利用）
- **YAML**: `deserialize()` で使用
- **shadcn/ui**: ダイアログ・ボタン等のUIコンポーネント
- **lucide-react**: アイコン

全ファイルは TypeScript strict mode で実装されており、型安全性が確保されています。

```
     1→import { parse } from "yaml";
     2→import type { FlowNode, FlowEdge } from "@/store/types";
     3→import type { FlowDirection, ComponentDefinition } from "@/types/flow";
     4→import type { FlowmaidLayout } from "./schema";
     5→import { idToCounter } from "@/lib/id";
     6→import { generateComponentChildren, generateBridgeEdges } from "@/lib/component-children";
     7→
     8→interface DeserializeResult {
     9→  nodes: FlowNode[];
    10→  edges: FlowEdge[];
    11→  direction: FlowDirection;
    12→  nextIdCounter: number;
    13→  componentDefinitions?: ComponentDefinition[];
    14→}
    15→
    16→export function deserialize(content: string): DeserializeResult {
    17→  const layoutMatch = content.indexOf("--- layout ---");
    18→  if (layoutMatch === -1) {
    19→    throw new Error("Invalid .flowmaid file: missing layout section");
    20→  }
    21→
    22→  const layoutYaml = content.slice(layoutMatch + "--- layout ---".length).trim();
    23→  const layout: FlowmaidLayout = parse(layoutYaml);
    24→
    25→  const nodes: FlowNode[] = [];
    26→  let maxCounter = -1;
    27→
    28→  for (const [id, n] of Object.entries(layout.nodes)) {
    29→    const isComponentInstance = !!n.componentDefinitionId;
    30→    const node: FlowNode = {
    31→      id,
    32→      type: isComponentInstance ? "componentInstance" : n.shape,
    33→      position: n.position,
    34→      data: {
    35→        label: n.label,
    36→        shape: n.shape as FlowNode["data"]["shape"],
    37→      },
    38→      style: n.size ? { width: n.size.width, height: n.size.height } : undefined,
    39→    };
    40→
    41→    // Restore component instance fields
    42→    if (n.componentDefinitionId) node.data.componentDefinitionId = n.componentDefinitionId;
    43→    if (n.componentInstanceName) node.data.componentInstanceName = n.componentInstanceName;
    44→    if (n.componentSyncVersion !== undefined) node.data.componentSyncVersion = n.componentSyncVersion;
    45→    // componentLabelOverrides is deprecated — ignored on import
    46→    if (n.collapsed) node.data.collapsed = n.collapsed;
    47→    if (n.expandedSize) node.data.expandedSize = n.expandedSize;
    48→
    49→    // Restore style properties
    50→    if (n.fillColor) node.data.fillColor = n.fillColor;
    51→    if (n.fillOpacity !== undefined) node.data.fillOpacity = n.fillOpacity;
    52→    if (n.fillLightness !== undefined) node.data.fillLightness = n.fillLightness;
    53→    if (n.borderColor) node.data.borderColor = n.borderColor;
    54→    if (n.borderOpacity !== undefined) node.data.borderOpacity = n.borderOpacity;
    55→    if (n.borderLightness !== undefined) node.data.borderLightness = n.borderLightness;
    56→    if (n.borderWidth) node.data.borderWidth = n.borderWidth;
    57→    if (n.borderStyle) node.data.borderStyle = n.borderStyle;
    58→    if (n.zIndex !== undefined) node.zIndex = n.zIndex;
    59→    if (n.fontSize) node.data.fontSize = n.fontSize;
    60→    if (n.textColor) node.data.textColor = n.textColor;
    61→    if (n.textOpacity !== undefined) node.data.textOpacity = n.textOpacity;
    62→    if (n.textLightness !== undefined) node.data.textLightness = n.textLightness;
    63→    if (n.textAlign) node.data.textAlign = n.textAlign;
    64→    if (n.textVerticalAlign) node.data.textVerticalAlign = n.textVerticalAlign;
    65→    if (n.bold) node.data.bold = n.bold;
    66→    if (n.italic) node.data.italic = n.italic;
    67→    if (n.underline) node.data.underline = n.underline;
    68→
    69→    nodes.push(node);
    70→
    71→    // Track highest counter for auto-id
    72→    if (/^[A-Z]+$/.test(id)) {
    73→      const counter = idToCounter(id);
    74→      if (counter > maxCounter) maxCounter = counter;
    75→    }
    76→  }
    77→
    78→  const edges: FlowEdge[] = [];
    79→  for (const [id, e] of Object.entries(layout.edges)) {
    80→    const edge: FlowEdge = {
    81→      id,
    82→      source: e.source,
    83→      target: e.target,
    84→      type: "labeled",
    85→      sourceHandle: e.sourceHandle,
    86→      targetHandle: e.targetHandle,
    87→      data: {
    88→        label: e.label ?? "",
    89→        edgeType: e.edgeType ?? "bezier",
    90→        markerStart: e.markerStart,
    91→        markerEnd: e.markerEnd ?? "arrowclosed",
    92→        strokeWidth: e.strokeWidth,
    93→        strokeColor: e.strokeColor,
    94→        strokeOpacity: e.strokeOpacity,
    95→        strokeLightness: e.strokeLightness,
    96→        strokeStyle: e.strokeStyle,
    97→        waypoints: e.waypoints,
    98→      },
    99→    };
   100→
   101→    edges.push(edge);
   102→  }
   103→
   104→  // Regenerate child nodes and internal edges for component instances
   105→  const componentDefinitions = layout.componentDefinitions ?? [];
   106→
   107→  const allChildNodes: FlowNode[] = [];
   108→  const allChildEdges: FlowEdge[] = [];
   109→
   110→  for (const node of nodes) {
   111→    if (!node.data.componentDefinitionId) continue;
   112→    const def = componentDefinitions.find((d) => d.id === node.data.componentDefinitionId);
   113→    if (!def) continue;
   114→
   115→    // Populate runtime-only direction field from definition
   116→    if (def.direction) {
   117→      node.data.componentDefinitionDirection = def.direction;
   118→    }
   119→
   120→    const isCollapsed = node.data.collapsed ?? false;
   121→
   122→    const { childNodes, childEdges } = generateComponentChildren({
   123→      parentId: node.id,
   124→      def,
   125→      hidden: isCollapsed,
   126→    });
   127→
   128→    allChildNodes.push(...childNodes);
   129→    allChildEdges.push(...childEdges);
   130→  }
   131→
   132→  // Generate bridge edges for component instances
   133→  const allNodes = [...nodes, ...allChildNodes];
   134→  const allEdgesBeforeBridge = [...edges, ...allChildEdges];
   135→  const bridgeEdges: FlowEdge[] = [];
   136→
   137→  for (const node of nodes) {
   138→    if (!node.data.componentDefinitionId) continue;
   139→    const def = componentDefinitions.find((d) => d.id === node.data.componentDefinitionId);
   140→    if (!def) continue;
   141→
   142→    const bridges = generateBridgeEdges({
   143→      parentId: node.id,
   144→      def,
   145→      allEdges: allEdgesBeforeBridge,
   146→      direction: def.direction ?? layout.direction,
   147→    });
   148→
   149→    const isCollapsed = node.data.collapsed ?? false;
   150→    for (const b of bridges) {
   151→      if (isCollapsed) b.hidden = true;
   152→      bridgeEdges.push(b);
   153→    }
   154→  }
   155→
   156→  return {
   157→    nodes: allNodes,
   158→    edges: [...allEdgesBeforeBridge, ...bridgeEdges],
   159→    direction: layout.direction ?? "TD",
   160→    nextIdCounter: maxCounter + 1,
   161→    componentDefinitions,
   162→  };
   163→}
   164→
```

    35→import { useLocale } from "@/lib/i18n/useLocale";
    36→import type { FlowDirection } from "@/types/flow";
    37→import type { TranslationKey } from "@/lib/i18n/locales";
    38→
    39→const SHAPE_KEYS: { type: string; key: TranslationKey }[] = [
    40→  { type: "rectangle", key: "rectangle" },
    41→  { type: "roundedRect", key: "roundedRect" },
    42→  { type: "diamond", key: "diamond" },
    43→  { type: "circle", key: "circle" },
    44→  { type: "stadium", key: "stadium" },
    45→  { type: "parallelogram", key: "parallelogram" },
    46→  { type: "cylinder", key: "cylinder" },

    48→  { type: "trapezoid", key: "trapezoid" },
    49→];
    50→
    51→interface ToolbarProps {
    52→  onExport?: () => void;
    53→  onImport?: () => void;
    54→  onImportMermaid?: () => void;
    55→  onFitView?: () => void;
    56→  isBulkEditMode?: boolean;
    57→  onEnterBulkEdit?: () => void;
    58→  onExitBulkEdit?: () => void;
    59→}
    60→
    61→export function Toolbar({ onExport, onImport, onImportMermaid, onFitView, isBulkEditMode, onEnterBulkEdit, onExitBulkEdit }: ToolbarProps) {
    62→  const { theme, setTheme } = useTheme();
    63→  const { undo, redo, canUndo, canRedo } = useUndoRedo();
    64→  const direction = useFlowStore((s) => s.direction);
    65→  const setDirection = useFlowStore((s) => s.setDirection);
    66→  const addNode = useFlowStore((s) => s.addNode);
    67→  const clearAll = useFlowStore((s) => s.clearAll);
    68→  const hasContent = useFlowStore((s) => s.nodes.length > 0 || s.edges.length > 0);
    69→  const { locale, setLocale, t } = useLocale();
    70→
    71→  return (
    72→    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1.5 border-b border-border bg-background">
    73→      <span className="font-semibold text-sm mr-2">Flowmaid</span>
    74→
    75→      <Separator orientation="vertical" className="h-6" />
    76→
    77→      <DropdownMenu>
    78→        <Tooltip>
    79→          <TooltipTrigger asChild>
    80→            <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
    81→              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-8 w-8">
    82→                <Plus size={16} />
    83→              </Button>
    84→            </DropdownMenuTrigger>
    85→          </TooltipTrigger>
    86→          <TooltipContent>{t("addNode")}</TooltipContent>
    87→        </Tooltip>
    88→        <DropdownMenuContent>
    89→          {SHAPE_KEYS.map(({ type, key }) => (
    90→            <DropdownMenuItem key={type} onClick={() => addNode(type)}>
    91→              {t(key)}
    92→            </DropdownMenuItem>
    93→          ))}
    94→        </DropdownMenuContent>
    95→      </DropdownMenu>
    96→
    97→      <Separator orientation="vertical" className="h-6" />
    98→
    99→      <Tooltip>
   100→        <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   101→          <Button
   102→            variant="ghost"
   103→            size="icon"
   104→            className="h-8 w-8"
   105→            onClick={undo}
   106→            disabled={!canUndo}
   107→          >
   108→            <Undo2 size={16} />
   109→          </Button>
   110→        </TooltipTrigger>
   111→        <TooltipContent>{t("undo")}</TooltipContent>
   112→      </Tooltip>
   113→
   114→      <Tooltip>
   115→        <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   116→          <Button
   117→            variant="ghost"
   118→            size="icon"
   119→            className="h-8 w-8"
   120→            onClick={redo}
   121→            disabled={!canRedo}
   122→          >
   123→            <Redo2 size={16} />
   124→          </Button>
   125→        </TooltipTrigger>
   126→        <TooltipContent>{t("redo")}</TooltipContent>
   127→      </Tooltip>
   128→
   129→      <Separator orientation="vertical" className="h-6" />
   130→
   131→      <ToggleGroup
   132→        type="single"
   133→        value={direction}
   134→        onValueChange={(val) => {
   135→          if (val) setDirection(val as FlowDirection);
   136→        }}
   137→        className="h-8"
   138→      >
   139→        <ToggleGroupItem value="TD" className="h-8 px-2 text-xs">
   140→          {t("dirTD")}
   141→        </ToggleGroupItem>
   142→        <ToggleGroupItem value="LR" className="h-8 px-2 text-xs">
   143→          {t("dirLR")}
   144→        </ToggleGroupItem>
   145→      </ToggleGroup>
   146→
   147→      <div className="flex-1" />
   148→
   149→      {onEnterBulkEdit && !isBulkEditMode && (
   150→        <Button
   151→          variant="outline"
   152→          size="sm"
   153→          className="h-7 gap-1.5 text-xs px-3"
   154→          onClick={onEnterBulkEdit}
   155→          disabled={!hasContent}
   156→        >
   157→          <Table2 size={14} />
   158→          {t("bulkEdit")}
   159→        </Button>
   160→      )}
   161→
   162→      <div className="flex-1" />
   163→
   164→      {onExport && (
   165→        <Tooltip>
   166→          <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   167→            <Button
   168→              variant="ghost"
   169→              size="icon"
   170→              className="h-8 w-8"
   171→              onClick={onExport}
   172→            >
   173→              <Upload size={16} />
   174→            </Button>
   175→          </TooltipTrigger>
   176→          <TooltipContent>{t("exportFile")}</TooltipContent>
   177→        </Tooltip>
   178→      )}
   179→
   180→      {onImport && (
   181→        <Tooltip>
   182→          <TooltipTrigger asChild>

   184→              variant="ghost"

   186→              className="h-8 w-8"
   187→              onClick={onImport}

   190→            </Button>
   191→          </TooltipTrigger>

   195→
   196→      {onImportMermaid && (
   197→        <Tooltip>
   198→          <TooltipTrigger asChild>

   200→              variant="ghost"
   201→              size="icon"
   202→              className="h-8 w-8"
   203→              onClick={onImportMermaid}
   204→            >
   205→              <FileCode2 size={16} />
   206→            </Button>
   207→          </TooltipTrigger>
   208→          <TooltipContent>{t("importMermaid")}</TooltipContent>
   209→        </Tooltip>
   210→      )}
   211→
   212→      <Tooltip>
   213→        <TooltipTrigger asChild>
   214→          <Button
   215→            variant="ghost"
   216→            size="icon"
   217→            className="h-8 w-8"
   218→            disabled={!hasContent}

   222→          >

   226→        <TooltipContent>{t("clearAll")}</TooltipContent>
   227→      </Tooltip>
   228→
   229→      <Separator orientation="vertical" className="h-6" />

    39→/** Resizable handle with click-to-toggle. Drag = resize, click = collapse panel.
    40→ *  Design matches PanelRibbon (w-6, bg-muted/50, border-x, chevron + vertical label). */
    41→function ToggleResizeHandle({ onClick }: { onClick: () => void }) {
    42→  const draggedRef = useRef(false);
    43→  return (
    44→    <div
    45→      className="relative shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 bg-muted/50 hover:bg-muted border-x border-border cursor-pointer transition-colors"
    46→      style={{ width: 24 }}
    47→      onPointerDown={() => { draggedRef.current = false; }}
    48→      onPointerMove={() => { draggedRef.current = true; }}
    49→      onPointerUp={() => {
    50→        if (!draggedRef.current) onClick();
    51→      }}
    52→    >
    53→      <ResizableHandle bare className="absolute inset-0 z-10 cursor-pointer" />
    54→      <ChevronRight size={14} className="text-muted-foreground" />
    55→      <span
    56→        className="text-[10px] text-muted-foreground"
    57→        style={{ writingMode: "vertical-rl", textOrientation: "mixed" }}
    58→      >
    59→        Output
    60→      </span>
    61→    </div>
    62→  );
    63→}
    64→
    65→/** Thin ribbon shown when panel is collapsed. Click to expand. Matches PanelRibbon design. */
    66→function ToggleRibbon({ onClick }: { onClick: () => void }) {
    67→  return (
    68→    <button
    69→      onClick={onClick}
    70→      className="shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center w-6 h-full bg-muted/50 hover:bg-muted border-x border-border cursor-pointer transition-colors"
    71→    >
    72→      <ChevronLeft size={14} className="text-muted-foreground" />
    73→      <span

    75→        style={{ writingMode: "vertical-rl", textOrientation: "mixed" }}
    76→      >
    77→        Output
    78→      </span>
    79→    </button>
    80→  );
    81→}

    83→export function EditorLayout() {
    84→  perfCount("EditorLayout");
    85→  const { leftOpen, rightOpen, leftWidth, toggleLeft, toggleRight } =
    86→    usePanelState();
    87→  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    88→  const [mermaidImportOpen, setMermaidImportOpen] = useState(false);
    89→  const [exportDialogOpen, setExportDialogOpen] = useState(false);

    91→  const [bulkEditFocusTarget, setBulkEditFocusTarget] = useState<{
    92→    type: "node" | "edge";
    93→    id: string;
    94→  } | null>(null);
    95→  const [bulkEditHighlightId, setBulkEditHighlightId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    96→  const [bulkEditSelectedIds, setBulkEditSelectedIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
    97→  const { t } = useLocale();
    98→  const isEditingComponent = useFlowStore((s) => !!s.editingComponentId);
    99→
   100→  useAutoSave();
   101→  useKeyboardShortcuts();
   102→
   103→  // Listen for export/import events
   104→  useEffect(() => {
   105→    const handleExportEvent = () => {
   106→      setExportDialogOpen(true);
   107→    };
   108→
   109→    const handleImportEvent = (e: Event) => {
   110→      try {
   111→        const content = (e as CustomEvent).detail as string;
   112→        const result = deserialize(content);
   113→        useFlowStore.getState().loadState(result);
   114→        toast.success(locales[useLocaleStore.getState().locale]["importedSuccess"]);
   115→      } catch (err) {
   116→        toast.error(locales[useLocaleStore.getState().locale]["importFailed"]);
   117→        console.error(err);
   118→      }
   119→    };
   120→
   121→    window.addEventListener("flowmaid:export", handleExportEvent);
   122→    window.addEventListener("flowmaid:import", handleImportEvent);
   123→    return () => {
   124→      window.removeEventListener("flowmaid:export", handleExportEvent);
   125→      window.removeEventListener("flowmaid:import", handleImportEvent);
   126→    };
   127→  }, []);
   128→
   129→  const handleExport = useCallback(() => {
   130→    setExportDialogOpen(true);
   131→  }, []);
   132→
   133→  const handleImport = useCallback(() => {
   134→    fileInputRef.current?.click();
   135→  }, []);
   136→
   137→  const handleFileChange = useCallback(
   138→    (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
   139→      const file = e.target.files?.[0];
   140→      if (!file) return;
   141→      const reader = new FileReader();
   142→      reader.onload = (ev) => {
   143→        const content = ev.target?.result as string;
   144→        window.dispatchEvent(
   145→          new CustomEvent("flowmaid:import", { detail: content })
   146→        );
   147→      };
   148→      reader.readAsText(file);
   149→      e.target.value = "";
   150→    },
   151→    []
   152→  );
   153→
   154→  const handleImportMermaid = useCallback(() => {
   155→    setMermaidImportOpen(true);
   156→  }, []);
   157→
   158→  const handleFitView = useCallback(() => {
   159→    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("flowmaid:fitview"));
   160→  }, []);
   161→
   162→  const handleEnterBulkEdit = useCallback(() => {
   163→    setBulkEditFocusTarget(null);
   164→    setBulkEditHighlightId(null);
   165→    setBulkEditSelectedIds([]);
   166→    setIsBulkEditMode(true);
   167→  }, []);
   168→
   169→  const handleExitBulkEdit = useCallback(() => {
   170→    setIsBulkEditMode(false);
   171→    setBulkEditFocusTarget(null);
   172→    setBulkEditHighlightId(null);
   173→    setBulkEditSelectedIds([]);
   174→  }, []);
   175→
   176→  // Table row click → highlight + zoom
   177→  const handleBulkEditFocusNode = useCallback((nodeId: string) => {
   178→    setBulkEditSelectedIds([]);
   179→    setBulkEditFocusTarget({ type: "node", id: nodeId });
   180→    setBulkEditHighlightId(nodeId);
   181→  }, []);
   182→
   183→  const handleBulkEditFocusEdge = useCallback((edgeId: string) => {
   184→    setBulkEditSelectedIds([]);
   185→    setBulkEditFocusTarget({ type: "edge", id: edgeId });
   186→    setBulkEditHighlightId(edgeId);
   187→  }, []);
   188→
   189→  // Canvas click → highlight only (no zoom)
   190→  const handleBulkEditCanvasNodeClick = useCallback((nodeId: string) => {
   191→    setBulkEditSelectedIds([]);
   192→    setBulkEditFocusTarget(null);
   193→    setBulkEditHighlightId(nodeId);
   194→  }, []);
   195→
   196→  const handleBulkEditCanvasEdgeClick = useCallback((edgeId: string) => {
   197→    setBulkEditSelectedIds([]);
   198→    setBulkEditFocusTarget(null);
   199→    setBulkEditHighlightId(edgeId);
   200→  }, []);
   201→
   202→  const handleBulkEditSelectionChange = useCallback((selectedIds: string[]) => {
   203→    setBulkEditSelectedIds(selectedIds);
   204→    if (selectedIds.length > 0) {
   205→      setBulkEditHighlightId(null);
   206→      setBulkEditFocusTarget(null);
   207→    }
   208→  }, []);
   209→
   210→  const handleBulkEditPaneClick = useCallback(() => {
   211→    setBulkEditSelectedIds([]);
   212→    setBulkEditHighlightId(null);
   213→    setBulkEditFocusTarget(null);
   214→  }, []);
   215→
   216→  return (
   217→    <ReactFlowProvider>
   218→      <DnDProvider>
   219→        <div
   220→          className="h-screen w-screen overflow-hidden flex flex-col transition-[padding,background-color] duration-500 ease-in-out"
   221→          style={{
   222→            padding: isEditingComponent ? '0.4% 1% 1% 1%' : '0',
   223→            backgroundColor: isEditingComponent ? 'var(--foreground)' : 'transparent',
   224→          }}
   225→        >
   226→          <div
   227→            className="overflow-hidden transition-[max-height,opacity] duration-500 ease-in-out"
   228→            style={{
   229→              maxHeight: isEditingComponent ? '48px' : '0px',
   230→              opacity: isEditingComponent ? 1 : 0,
   231→            }}
   232→          >
   233→            <ComponentEditingHeader />
   234→          </div>
   235→          <div className={`flex-1 min-h-0 w-full flex flex-col transition-[border-radius] duration-500 ease-in-out overflow-hidden ${isEditingComponent ? 'rounded-lg bg-background' : ''}`}>
   236→          <Toolbar
   237→            onExport={isBulkEditMode ? undefined : handleExport}
   238→            onImport={isBulkEditMode ? undefined : handleImport}
   239→            onImportMermaid={isBulkEditMode ? undefined : handleImportMermaid}
   240→            onFitView={isBulkEditMode ? undefined : handleFitView}
   241→            isBulkEditMode={isBulkEditMode}
   242→            onEnterBulkEdit={isEditingComponent ? undefined : handleEnterBulkEdit}
   243→            onExitBulkEdit={handleExitBulkEdit}
   244→          />
   245→          {!isBulkEditMode && <FormatBar />}
   246→          {isBulkEditMode ? (
   247→            <div className="flex flex-1 overflow-hidden">
   248→              <ResizablePanelGroup orientation="horizontal" className="flex-1">
   249→                <ResizablePanel defaultSize={BULK_EDIT_CANVAS_DEFAULT_SIZE} minSize={BULK_EDIT_CANVAS_MIN_SIZE}>
   250→                  <BulkEditCanvas
   251→                    focusTarget={bulkEditFocusTarget}
   252→                    highlightId={bulkEditHighlightId}
   253→                    onNodeClick={handleBulkEditCanvasNodeClick}
   254→                    onEdgeClick={handleBulkEditCanvasEdgeClick}
   255→                    onSelectionChange={handleBulkEditSelectionChange}
   256→                    onPaneClick={handleBulkEditPaneClick}
   257→                    onExit={handleExitBulkEdit}
   258→                  />
   259→                </ResizablePanel>
   260→                <ResizableHandle bare className="w-px bg-border" />
   261→                <ResizablePanel defaultSize={BULK_EDIT_TABLE_DEFAULT_SIZE} minSize={BULK_EDIT_TABLE_MIN_SIZE}>
   262→                  <BulkEditTable
   263→                    onFocusNode={handleBulkEditFocusNode}
   264→                    onFocusEdge={handleBulkEditFocusEdge}
   265→                    highlightId={bulkEditHighlightId}
   266→                    selectedIds={bulkEditSelectedIds}
   267→                  />
   268→                </ResizablePanel>
   269→              </ResizablePanelGroup>
   270→            </div>

   278→              ribbonLabel="Nodes"

   283→            <ResizablePanelGroup orientation="horizontal" className="flex-1">
   284→              <ResizablePanel defaultSize={rightOpen ? 70 : 100} minSize={30}>

   287→
   288→              {rightOpen && (

   294→                </>

   297→
   298→            {!rightOpen && (
   299→              <ToggleRibbon onClick={toggleRight} />
   300→            )}
   301→          </div>
   302→          )}
   303→          </div>
   304→        </div>
   305→        <input

   320→        />
   321→        <BetaNoticeDialog />

for edges */\nconst EDGE_STYLE_FIELDS: (keyof FlowmaidEdgeLayout)[] = [\n  \"edgeType\", \"markerStart\", \"markerEnd\",\n  \"strokeWidth\", \"strokeColor\", \"strokeOpacity\", \"strokeLightness\",\n  \"strokeStyle\",\n];\n\nfunction deepEqual(a: unknown, b: unknown): boolean {\n  if (a === b) return true;\n  if (a == null || b == null) return a == b;\n  if (typeof a !== typeof b) return false;\n  if (typeof a !== \"object\") return false;\n  const keysA = Object.keys(a as Record<string, unknown>);\n  const keysB = Object.keys(b as Record<string, unknown>);\n  if (keysA.length !== keysB.length) return false;\n  for (const key of keysA) {\n    if (!deepEqual((a as Record<string, unknown>)[key], (b as Record<string, unknown>)[key])) {\n      return false;\n    }\n  }\n  return true;\n}\n\nfunction getChangedFields<T extends Record<string, unknown>>(\n  base: T,\n  compare: T,\n  fields: (keyof T)[]\n): string[] {\n  const changed: string[] = [];\n  for (const field of fields) {\n    if (!deepEqual(base[field], compare[field])) {\n      changed.push(field as string);\n    }\n  }\n  return changed;\n}\n\nfunction computeNodeDiffs(base: FlowmaidLayout, compare: FlowmaidLayout): NodeDiff[] {\n  const diffs: NodeDiff[] = [];\n  const baseNodes = base.nodes;\n  const compareNodes = compare.nodes;\n\n  const allIds = new Set([...Object.keys(baseNodes), ...Object.keys(compareNodes)]);\n\n  for (const id of allIds) {\n    const baseNode = baseNodes[id];\n    const compareNode = compareNodes[id];\n\n    if (!baseNode) {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"added\", nodeId: id, compareNode, changedFields: [] });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (!compareNode) {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"deleted\", nodeId: id, baseNode, changedFields: [] });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const textChanged = getChangedFields(\n      baseNode as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,\n      compareNode as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,\n      TEXT_FIELDS as unknown as string[]\n    );\n    const posChanged = getChangedFields(\n      baseNode as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,\n      compareNode as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,\n      POSITION_FIELDS as unknown as string[]\n    );\n    const styleChanged = getChangedFields(\n      baseNode as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,\n      compareNode as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,\n      NODE_STYLE_FIELDS as unknown as string[]\n    );\n\n    const allChanged = [...textChanged, ...posChanged, ...styleChanged];\n\n    if (allChanged.length === 0) {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"unchanged\", nodeId: id, baseNode, compareNode, changedFields: [] });\n    } else if (posChanged.length > 0 && textChanged.length === 0 && styleChanged.length === 0) {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"moved\", nodeId: id, baseNode, compareNode, changedFields: allChanged });\n    } else {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"modified\", nodeId: id, baseNode, compareNode, changedFields: allChanged });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return diffs;\n}\n\nfunction computeEdgeDiffs(base: FlowmaidLayout, compare: FlowmaidLayout): EdgeDiff[] {\n  const diffs: EdgeDiff[] = [];\n  const baseEdges = base.edges;\n  const compareEdges = compare.edges;\n\n  const allKeys = new Set([...Object.keys(baseEdges), ...Object.keys(compareEdges)]);\n\n  for (const key of allKeys) {\n    const baseEdge = baseEdges[key];\n    const compareEdge = compareEdges[key];\n\n    if (!baseEdge) {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"added\", edgeKey: key, compareEdge, changedFields: [] });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (!compareEdge) {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"deleted\", edgeKey: key, baseEdge, changedFields: [] });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const textChanged = getChangedFields(\n      baseEdge as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,\n      compareEdge as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,\n      EDGE_TEXT_FIELDS as unknown as string[]\n    );\n    const styleChanged = getChangedFields(\n      baseEdge as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,\n      compareEdge as unknown as Record<string, unknown>,\n      EDGE_STYLE_FIELDS as unknown as string[]\n    );\n\n    const allChanged = [...textChanged, ...styleChanged];\n\n    if (allChanged.length === 0) {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"unchanged\", edgeKey: key, baseEdge, compareEdge, changedFields: [] });\n    } else {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"modified\", edgeKey: key, baseEdge, compareEdge, changedFields: allChanged });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return diffs;\n}\n\nfunction computeComponentDefDiffs(\n  baseDefs: ComponentDefinition[],\n  compareDefs: ComponentDefinition[]\n): ComponentDefDiff[] {\n  const diffs: ComponentDefDiff[] = [];\n  const baseMap = new Map(baseDefs.map((d) => [d.id, d]));\n  const compareMap = new Map(compareDefs.map((d) => [d.id, d]));\n\n  const allIds = new Set([...baseMap.keys(), ...compareMap.keys()]);\n\n  for (const id of allIds) {\n    const baseDef = baseMap.get(id);\n    const compareDef = compareMap.get(id);\n\n    if (!baseDef) {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"added\", defId: id, compareDef, changedFields: [] });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (!compareDef) {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"deleted\", defId: id, baseDef, changedFields: [] });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const changedFields: string[] = [];\n    if (baseDef.name !== compareDef.name) changedFields.push(\"name\");\n    if (baseDef.entryNodeId !== compareDef.entryNodeId) changedFields.push(\"entryNodeId\");\n    if (baseDef.exitNodeId !== compareDef.exitNodeId) changedFields.push(\"exitNodeId\");\n    if (!deepEqual(baseDef.nodes, compareDef.nodes)) changedFields.push(\"nodes\");\n    if (!deepEqual(baseDef.edges, compareDef.edges)) changedFields.push(\"edges\");\n    if (baseDef.direction !== compareDef.direction) changedFields.push(\"direction\");\n\n    if (changedFields.length === 0) {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"unchanged\", defId: id, baseDef, compareDef, changedFields: [] });\n    } else {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"modified\", defId: id, baseDef, compareDef, changedFields });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return diffs;\n}\n\n/**\n * Compute differences between two .flowmaid layouts.\n * Pure function — no side effects or external state dependencies.\n */\nexport function computeDiff(base: FlowmaidLayout, compare: FlowmaidLayout): DiffResult {\n  return {\n    nodeDiffs: computeNodeDiffs(base, compare),\n    edgeDiffs: computeEdgeDiffs(base, compare),\n    componentDefDiffs: computeComponentDefDiffs(\n      base.componentDefinitions ?? [],\n      compare.componentDefinitions ?? []\n    ),\n  };\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import type { DiffResult, DiffFilters, NodeDiff, EdgeDiff, ComponentDefDiff } from \"./types\";\n\nexport interface DiffTextLine {\n  type: \"header\" | \"added\" | \"deleted\" | \"context\" | \"section\";\n  text: string;\n  /** Node or edge ID for click-to-flash */\n  targetId?: string;\n  targetType?: \"node\" | \"edge\" | \"componentDef\";\n}\n\nfunction formatValue(value: unknown): string {\n  if (value == null) return \"undefined\";\n  if (typeof value === \"object\") {\n    return JSON.stringify(value);\n  }\n  return String(value);\n}\n\nfunction isTextChange(field: string): boolean {\n  return [\"label\", \"shape\"].includes(field);\n}\n\nfunction isPositionChange(field: string): boolean {\n  return field === \"position\";\n}\n\nfunction isStyleChange(field: string): boolean {\n  return !isTextChange(field) && !isPositionChange(field);\n}\n\nfunction shouldShowField(field: string, filters: DiffFilters): boolean {\n  if (isTextChange(field) && !filters.textChanges) return false;\n  if (isPositionChange(field) && !filters.positionChanges) return false;\n  if (isStyleChange(field) && !filters.styleChanges) return false;\n  return true;\n}\n\nfunction formatNodeDiff(diff: NodeDiff, filters: DiffFilters): DiffTextLine[] {\n  const lines: DiffTextLine[] = [];\n  const id = diff.nodeId;\n\n  if (diff.kind === \"added\" && diff.compareNode) {\n    lines.push({ type: \"header\", text: `[+] ${id}  Added`, targetId: id, targetType: \"node\" });\n    lines.push({ type: \"added\", text: `    shape: ${diff.compareNode.shape}` });\n    lines.push({ type: \"added\", text: `    label: \"${diff.compareNode.label}\"` });\n    lines.push({ type: \"added\", text: `    position: { x: ${diff.compareNode.position.x}, y: ${diff.compareNode.position.y} }` });\n    return lines;\n  }\n\n  if (diff.kind === \"deleted\" && diff.baseNode) {\n    lines.push({ type: \"header\", text: `[-] ${id}  Deleted`, targetId: id, targetType: \"node\" });\n    lines.push({ type: \"deleted\", text: `    shape: ${diff.baseNode.shape}` });\n    lines.push({ type: \"deleted\", text: `    label: \"${diff.baseNode.label}\"` });\n    return lines;\n  }\n\n  if ((diff.kind === \"modified\" || diff.kind === \"moved\") && diff.baseNode && diff.compareNode) {\n    const filteredFields = diff.changedFields.filter((f) => shouldShowField(f, filters));\n    if (filteredFields.length === 0) return lines;\n\n    const kindLabel = diff.kind === \"moved\" ? \"Moved\" : \"Modified\";\n    lines.push({ type: \"header\", text: `[~] ${id}  ${kindLabel}`, targetId: id, targetType: \"node\" });\n\n    for (const field of filteredFields) {\n      const baseVal = (diff.baseNode as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[field];\n      const compareVal = (diff.compareNode as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[field];\n      lines.push({ type: \"deleted\", text: `  - ${field}: ${formatValue(baseVal)}` });\n      lines.push({ type: \"added\", text: `  + ${field}: ${formatValue(compareVal)}` });\n    }\n\n    return lines;\n  }\n\n  return lines;\n}\n\nfunction formatEdgeDiff(diff: EdgeDiff, filters: DiffFilters): DiffTextLine[] {\n  const lines: DiffTextLine[] = [];\n  const key = diff.edgeKey;\n  const displayKey = key.length > 30 ? key.slice(0, 30) + \"...\" : key;\n\n  if (diff.kind === \"added\" && diff.compareEdge) {\n    lines.push({ type: \"header\", text: `[+] Edge ${displayKey}  Added`, targetId: key, targetType: \"edge\" });\n    lines.push({ type: \"added\", text: `    ${diff.compareEdge.source} → ${diff.compareEdge.target}` });\n    if (diff.compareEdge.label) {\n      lines.push({ type: \"added\", text: `    label: \"${diff.compareEdge.label}\"` });\n    }\n    return lines;\n  }\n\n  if (diff.kind === \"deleted\" && diff.baseEdge) {\n    lines.push({ type: \"header\", text: `[-] Edge ${displayKey}  Deleted`, targetId: key, targetType: \"edge\" });\n    lines.push({ type: \"deleted\", text: `    ${diff.baseEdge.source} → ${diff.baseEdge.target}` });\n    return lines;\n  }\n\n  if (diff.kind === \"modified\" && diff.baseEdge && diff.compareEdge) {\n    const filteredFields = diff.changedFields.filter((f) => {\n      if ([\"label\"].includes(f) && !filters.textChanges) return false;\n      if (![\"label\"].includes(f) && !filters.styleChanges) return false;\n      return true;\n    });\n    if (filteredFields.length === 0) return lines;\n\n    lines.push({ type: \"header\", text: `[~] Edge ${displayKey}  Modified`, targetId: key, targetType: \"edge\" });\n    for (const field of filteredFields) {\n      const baseVal = (diff.baseEdge as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[field];\n      const compareVal = (diff.compareEdge as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[field];\n      lines.push({ type: \"deleted\", text: `  - ${field}: ${formatValue(baseVal)}` });\n      lines.push({ type: \"added\", text: `  + ${field}: ${formatValue(compareVal)}` });\n    }\n    return lines;\n  }\n\n  return lines;\n}\n\nfunction formatComponentDefDiff(diff: ComponentDefDiff): DiffTextLine[] {\n  const lines: DiffTextLine[] = [];\n  const id = diff.defId;\n\n  if (diff.kind === \"added\" && diff.compareDef) {\n    lines.push({ type: \"header\", text: `[+] Component \"${diff.compareDef.name}\"  Added`, targetId: id, targetType: \"componentDef\" });\n    lines.push({ type: \"added\", text: `    nodes: ${diff.compareDef.nodes.length}` });\n    lines.push({ type: \"added\", text: `    edges: ${diff.compareDef.edges.length}` });\n    return lines;\n  }\n\n  if (diff.kind === \"deleted\" && diff.baseDef) {\n    lines.push({ type: \"header\", text: `[-] Component \"${diff.baseDef.name}\"  Deleted`, targetId: id, targetType: \"componentDef\" });\n    return lines;\n  }\n\n  if (diff.kind === \"modified\" && diff.baseDef && diff.compareDef) {\n    lines.push({ type: \"header\", text: `[~] Component \"${diff.baseDef.name}\"  Modified`, targetId: id, targetType: \"componentDef\" });\n    for (const field of diff.changedFields) {\n      if (field === \"name\") {\n        lines.push({ type: \"deleted\", text: `  - name: \"${diff.baseDef.name}\"` });\n        lines.push({ type: \"added\", text: `  + name: \"${diff.compareDef.name}\"` });\n      } else if (field === \"nodes\") {\n        lines.push({ type: \"context\", text: `    nodes: ${diff.baseDef.nodes.length} → ${diff.compareDef.nodes.length}` });\n      } else if (field === \"edges\") {\n        lines.push({ type: \"context\", text: `    edges: ${diff.baseDef.edges.length} → ${diff.compareDef.edges.length}` });\n      } else {\n        lines.push({ type: \"context\", text: `    ${field}: changed` });\n      }\n    }\n    return lines;\n  }\n\n  return lines;\n}\n\n/**\n * Format DiffResult into git-diff-style text lines.\n * Pure function — filters control which diff categories to include.\n */\nexport function formatTextDiff(result: DiffResult, filters: DiffFilters): DiffTextLine[] {\n  const lines: DiffTextLine[] = [];\n\n  // Node diffs\n  const activeNodeDiffs = result.nodeDiffs.filter((d) => {\n    if (d.kind === \"unchanged\") return false;\n    if (d.kind === \"moved\" && !filters.positionChanges) return false;\n    return true;\n  });\n\n  if (activeNodeDiffs.length > 0) {\n    lines.push({ type: \"section\", text: \"--- Nodes ---\" });\n    for (const diff of activeNodeDiffs) {\n      const formatted = formatNodeDiff(diff, filters);\n      if (formatted.length > 0) {\n        lines.push(...formatted);\n        lines.push({ type: \"context\", text: \"\" });\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Edge diffs\n  if (filters.edges) {\n    const activeEdgeDiffs = result.edgeDiffs.filter((d) => d.kind !== \"unchanged\");\n    if (activeEdgeDiffs.length > 0) {\n      lines.push({ type: \"section\", text: \"--- Edges ---\" });\n      for (const diff of activeEdgeDiffs) {\n        const formatted = formatEdgeDiff(diff, filters);\n        if (formatted.length > 0) {\n          lines.push(...formatted);\n          lines.push({ type: \"context\", text: \"\" });\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Component definition diffs\n  const activeDefDiffs = result.componentDefDiffs.filter((d) => d.kind !== \"unchanged\");\n  if (activeDefDiffs.length > 0) {\n    lines.push({ type: \"section\", text: \"--- Components ---\" });\n    for (const diff of activeDefDiffs) {\n      const formatted = formatComponentDefDiff(diff);\n      if (formatted.length > 0) {\n        lines.push(...formatted);\n        lines.push({ type: \"context\", text: \"\" });\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return lines;\n}\n"
}

  "old_string": "export function deserialize(content: string): DeserializeResult {",
  "new_string": "/**\n * Parse a .flowmaid file and return only the raw layout data (no React Flow conversion).\n * Used by diff comparison to compare layouts directly without React Flow internal state.\n */\nexport function parseLayoutOnly(content: string): FlowmaidLayout {\n  const layoutMatch = content.indexOf(\"--- layout ---\");\n  if (layoutMatch === -1) {\n    throw new Error(\"Invalid .flowmaid file: missing layout section\");\n  }\n  const layoutYaml = content.slice(layoutMatch + \"--- layout ---\".length).trim();\n  const layout: FlowmaidLayout = parse(layoutYaml);\n  return layout;\n}\n\nexport function deserialize(content: string): DeserializeResult {"
}

size=\"sm\" onClick={onClear} className=\"gap-1.5 text-xs\">\n          <X size={14} />\n          {t(\"diffClear\")}\n        </Button>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-full gap-4 transition-colors ${\n        isDragOver ? \"bg-primary/5\" : \"\"\n      }`}\n      onDrop={handleDrop}\n      onDragOver={handleDragOver}\n      onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}\n    >\n      <h3 className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-muted-foreground\">{label}</h3>\n\n      <div\n        className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-3 p-8 rounded-lg border-2 border-dashed transition-colors ${\n          isDragOver ? \"border-primary bg-primary/5\" : \"border-muted-foreground/30\"\n        }`}\n      >\n        <Upload size={24} className=\"text-muted-foreground\" />\n        <Button\n          variant=\"outline\"\n          size=\"sm\"\n          onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}\n        >\n          {t(\"diffSelectFile\")}\n        </Button>\n        <span className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">{t(\"diffDropFile\")}</span>\n      </div>\n\n      {showUseCurrentCanvas && hasContent && (\n        <Button\n          variant=\"secondary\"\n          size=\"sm\"\n          onClick={handleUseCurrentCanvas}\n          className=\"gap-1.5 text-xs\"\n        >\n          {t(\"diffUseCurrentCanvas\")}\n        </Button>\n      )}\n\n      <input\n        ref={fileInputRef}\n        type=\"file\"\n        accept=\".flowmaid\"\n        className=\"hidden\"\n        onChange={handleFileInputChange}\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\ninterface DiffImportPanelProps {\n  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout | null;\n  baseFileName: string | null;\n  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout | null;\n  compareFileName: string | null;\n  onBaseSelect: (layout: FlowmaidLayout, fileName: string) => void;\n  onCompareSelect: (layout: FlowmaidLayout, fileName: string) => void;\n  onBaseClear: () => void;\n  onCompareClear: () => void;\n  onRunCompare: () => void;\n}\n\nexport function DiffImportPanel({\n  baseLayout,\n  baseFileName,\n  compareLayout,\n  compareFileName,\n  onBaseSelect,\n  onCompareSelect,\n  onBaseClear,\n  onCompareClear,\n  onRunCompare,\n}: DiffImportPanelProps) {\n  const { t } = useLocale();\n  const canCompare = baseLayout !== null && compareLayout !== null;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex flex-col h-full\">\n      <div className=\"flex flex-1 overflow-hidden\">\n        <div className=\"flex-1 border-r border-border\">\n          <FileSlot\n            label={t(\"diffSelectBase\")}\n            layout={baseLayout}\n            fileName={baseFileName}\n            onFileSelect={onBaseSelect}\n            onClear={onBaseClear}\n            showUseCurrentCanvas\n          />\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"flex-1\">\n          <FileSlot\n            label={t(\"diffSelectCompare\")}\n <div className=\"flex justify-center py-4 border-t border-border\">\n        <Button\n          onClick={onRunCompare}\n          disabled={!canCompare}\n          className=\"px-8\"\n        >\n          {t(\"diffRunCompare\")}\n        </Button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"

  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useMemo } from \"react\";\nimport type { DiffResult, DiffFilters } from \"@/lib/diff/types\";\nimport { formatTextDiff, type DiffTextLine } from \"@/lib/diff/formatTextDiff\";\nimport { useLocale } from \"@/lib/i18n/useLocale\";\n\ninterface DiffTextPanelProps {\n  diffResult: DiffResult;\n  filters: DiffFilters;\n  onItemClick: (targetId: string, targetType: \"node\" | \"edge\" | \"componentDef\") => void;\n}\n\nfunction DiffLine({ line, onClick }: { line: DiffTextLine; onClick?: () => void }) {\n  const baseClass = \"px-3 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs whitespace-pre\";\n  const isClickable = !!line.targetId && !!onClick;\n\n  switch (line.type) {\n    case \"section\":\n      return (\n        <div className={`${baseClass} font-semibold text-muted-foreground bg-muted/50 py-1.5 mt-2 first:mt-0`}>\n          {line.text}\n        </div>\n      );\n    case \"header\":\n      return (\n        <div\n          className={`${baseClass} font-semibold py-1 ${\n            line.text.includes(\"Added\") ? \"text-green-600 dark:text-green-400\" :\n            line.text.includes(\"Deleted\") ? \"text-red-600 dark:text-red-400\" :\n            \"text-yellow-600 dark:text-yellow-400\"\n          } ${isClickable ? \"cursor-pointer hover:bg-muted/50\" : \"\"}`}\n          onClick={onClick}\n        >\n          {line.text}\n        </div>\n      );\n    case \"added\":\n      return (\n        <div className={`${baseClass} text-green-600 dark:text-green-400 bg-green-500/5`}>\n          {line.text}\n        </div>\n      );\n    case \"deleted\":\n      return (\n        <div className={`${baseClass} text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 bg-red-500/5`}>\n          {line.text}\n        </div>\n      );\n    case \"context\":\n      return (\n        <div className={`${baseClass} text-muted-foreground`}>\n          {line.text || \"\\u00A0\"}\n        </div>\n      );\n    default:\n      return null;\n  }\n}\n\nexport function DiffTextPanel({ diffResult, filters, onItemClick }: DiffTextPanelProps) {\n  const { t } = useLocale();\n\n  const lines = useMemo(\n    () => formatTextDiff(diffResult, filters),\n    [diffResult, filters]\n  );\n\n  if (lines.length === 0) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center h-full text-muted-foreground text-sm\">\n        {t(\"diffNoDifferences\")}\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"h-full overflow-auto bg-background\">\n      <div className=\"py-2\">\n        {lines.map((line, i) => (\n          <DiffLine\n            key={i}\n            line={line}\n            onClick={\n              line.targetId && line.targetType\n                ? () => onItemClick(line.targetId!, line.targetType!)\n                : undefined\n            }\n          />\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

Node,\n  type Edge,\n} from \"@xyflow/react\";\nimport { ArrowLeft } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useLocale } from \"@/lib/i18n/useLocale\";\nimport { nodeTypes } from \"@/components/nodes/nodeTypes\";\nimport { edgeTypes } from \"@/components/edges/edgeTypes\";\nimport type { FlowmaidLayout } from \"@/lib/flowmaid/schema\";\nimport type { DiffResult, DiffFilters, DiffKind } from \"@/lib/diff/types\";\nimport { deserialize } from \"@/lib/flowmaid/deserialize\";\nimport { serialize } from \"@/lib/flowmaid/serialize\";\nimport type { FlowNode, FlowEdge } from \"@/store/types\";\n\n/** Distance threshold for grouping nearby changed nodes (px) */\nconst GROUPING_THRESHOLD = 100;\n\n/** Number of times to flash a node when clicked in text panel */\nconst FLASH_COUNT = 3;\nconst FLASH_INTERVAL_MS = 200;\n\ninterface DiffOverlayRect {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  width: number;\n  height: number;\n  kind: DiffKind;\n  label: string;\n  nodeIds: string[];\n}\n\n/** Padding around diff overlay rectangles */\nconst OVERLAY_PADDING = 12;\n\nfunction buildOverlayRects(\n  diffResult: DiffResult,\n  filters: DiffFilters,\n  nodes: Node[],\n  t: (key: string) => string,\n): DiffOverlayRect[] {\n  const rects: DiffOverlayRect[] = [];\n  const nodeMap = new Map(nodes.map((n) => [n.id, n]));\n\n  // Collect individual diff items with positions\n  const items: { id: string; kind: DiffKind; x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }[] = [];\n\n  for (const nd of diffResult.nodeDiffs) {\n    if (nd.kind === \"unchanged\") continue;\n    if (nd.kind === \"moved\" && !filters.positionChanges) continue;\n    if (nd.kind === \"modified\") {\n      const hasVisibleChanges = nd.changedFields.some((f) => {\n        if ([\"label\", \"shape\"].includes(f)) return filters.textChanges;\n        if (f === \"position\") return filters.positionChanges;\n        return filters.styleChanges;\n      });\n      if (!hasVisibleChanges) continue;\n    }\n\n    const node = nodeMap.get(nd.nodeId);\n    if (!node) continue;\n\n    const w = (node.measured?.width ?? node.width ?? 150) as number;\n    const h = (node.measured?.height ?? node.height ?? 50) as number;\n\n    items.push({ id: nd.nodeId, kind: nd.kind, x: node.position.x, y: node.position.y, w, h });\n  }\n\n  // Group nearby items of the same kind\n  const used = new Set<number>();\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {\n    if (used.has(i)) continue;\n\n    const group = [items[i]];\n    used.add(i);\n\n    // Find nearby items of the same kind\n    for (let j = i + 1; j < items.length; j++) {\n      if (used.has(j)) continue;\n      if (items[j].kind !== items[i].kind) continue;\n\n      // Check if any item in the group is close enough\n      const isClose = group.some((g) => {\n        const dx = Math.abs((items[j].x + items[j].w / 2) - (g.x + g.w / 2));\n        const dy = Math.abs((items[j].y + items[j].h / 2) - (g.y + g.h / 2));\n        return dx < GROUPING_THRESHOLD + g.w / 2 + items[j].w / 2 &&\n               dy < GROUPING_THRESHOLD + g.h / 2 + items[j].h / 2;\n      });\n\n      if (isClose) {\n        group.push(items[j]);\n        used.add(j);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Compute bounding box\n    let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;\n    const nodeIds: string[] = [];\n    for (const item of group) {\n      minX = Math.min(minX, item.x);\n      minY = Math.min(minY, item.y);\n      maxX = Math.max(maxX, item.x + item.w);\n      maxY = Math.max(maxY, item.y + item.h);\n      nodeIds.push(item.id);\n    }\n\n    const kindLabels: Record<DiffKind, string> = {\n      added: t(\"diffKindAdded\"),\n      deleted: t(\"diffKindDeleted\"),\n      modified: t(\"diffKindModified\"),\n      moved: t(\"diffKindMoved\"),\n      unchanged: \"\",\n    };\n\n    rects.push({\n      x: minX - OVERLAY_PADDING,\n      y: minY - OVERLAY_PADDING,\n      width: maxX - minX + OVERLAY_PADDING * 2,\n      height: maxY - minY + OVERLAY_PADDING * 2,\n      kind: group[0].kind,\n      label: kindLabels[group[0].kind],\n      nodeIds,\n    });\n  }\n\n  return rects;\n}\n\nconst DIFF_COLORS: Record<DiffKind, string> = {\n  added: \"#22c55e\",\n  deleted: \"#ef4444\",\n  modified: \"#eab308\",\n  moved: \"#3b82f6\",\n  unchanged: \"transparent\",\n};\n\ninterface DiffCanvasInnerProps {\n  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  diffResult: DiffResult;\n  filters: DiffFilters;\n  flashTargetId: string | null;\n  onExit: () => void;\n}\n\nconst DiffCanvasInner = memo(function DiffCanvasInner({\n  baseLayout,\n  compareLayout,\n  diffResult,\n  filters,\n  flashTargetId,\n  onExit,\n}: DiffCanvasInnerProps) {\n  const { t } = useLocale();\n  const { getNodes, fitView } = useReactFlow();\n  const [overlayRects, setOverlayRects] = useState<DiffOverlayRect[]>([]);\n  const [flashingIds, setFlashingIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());\n\n  // Convert layouts to React Flow nodes/edges for display\n  const { displayNodes, displayEdges } = useMemo(() => {\n    // Serialize base layout back to .flowmaid content, then deserialize to FlowNode/FlowEdge\n    const baseContent = `--- mermaid ---\\ngraph TD\\n\\n--- layout ---\\n${JSON.stringify(baseLayout)}`;\n    // We need proper YAML serialization, so we'll construct nodes/edges manually\n    const nodes: FlowNode[] = [];\n    const edges: FlowEdge[] = [];\n\n    // Build nodes from base layout\n    for (const [id, n] of Object.entries(baseLayout.nodes)) {\n      const nd = diffResult.nodeDiffs.find((d) => d.nodeId === id);\n      const isDeleted = nd?.kind === \"deleted\";\n\n      nodes.push({\n        id,\n        type: n.componentDefinitionId ? \"componentInstance\" : n.shape,\n        position: n.position,\n        data: {\n          label: n.label,\n          shape: n.shape as FlowNode[\"data\"][\"shape\"],\n          fillColor: n.fillColor,\n          borderColor: n.borderColor,\n          borderWidth: n.borderWidth,\n          borderStyle: n.borderStyle,\n          fontSize: n.fontSize,\n          textColor: n.textColor,\n          textAlign: n.textAlign,\n          bold: n.bold,\n          italic: n.italic,\n          underline: n.underline,\n        },\n        style: {\n          ...(n.size ? { width: n.size.width, height: n.size.height } : {}),\n          ...(isDeleted ? { opacity: 0.4 } : {}),\n        },\n      } as FlowNode);\n    }\n\n    // Add \"added\" nodes from compare layout\n    for (const nd of diffResult.nodeDiffs) {\n      if (nd.kind !== \"added\" || !nd.compareNode) continue;\n      const n = nd.compareNode;\n      nodes.push({\n        id: nd.nodeId,\n        type: n.componentDefinitionId ? \"componentInstance\" : n.shape,\n        position: n.position,\n        data: {\n          label: n.label,\n          shape: n.shape as FlowNode[\"data\"][\"shape\"],\n          fillColor: n.fillColor,\n          borderColor: n.borderColor,\n        },\n        style: n.size ? { width: n.size.width, height: n.size.height } : undefined,\n      } as FlowNode);\n    }\n\n    // Add \"moved\" ghost nodes (old position, semi-transparent)\n    for (const nd of diffResult.nodeDiffs) {\n      if (nd.kind !== \"moved\" || !nd.baseNode || !nd.compareNode) continue;\n      if (!filters.positionChanges) continue;\n      // The base position node is already in (from base layout)\n      // We need to show the compare position as the \"new\" position\n      // Update the existing base node to be semi-transparent (old position)\n      const existing = nodes.find((n) => n.id === nd.nodeId);\n      if (existing) {\n        existing.style = { ...existing.style, opacity: 0.4 };\n      }\n      // Add a new node at compare position\n      const n = nd.compareNode;\n      nodes.push({\n        id: `${nd.nodeId}__moved`,\n        type: n.componentDefinitionId ? \"componentInstance\" : n.shape,\n        position: n.position,\n        data: {\n          label: n.label,\n          shape: n.shape as FlowNode[\"data\"][\"shape\"],\n          fillColor: n.fillColor,\n          borderColor: n.borderColor,\n        },\n        style: n.size ? { width: n.size.width, height: n.size.height } : undefined,\n      } as FlowNode);\n    }\n\n    // Build edges from base layout\n    for (const [id, e] of Object.entries(baseLayout.edges)) {\n      if (!filters.edges) continue;\n      const ed = diffResult.edgeDiffs.find((d) => d.edgeKey === id);\n      const isDeleted = ed?.kind === \"deleted\";\n\n      edges.push({\n        id,\n        source: e.source,\n        target: e.target,\n        type: \"labeled\",\n        sourceHandle: e.sourceHandle,\n        targetHandle: e.targetHandle,\n        data: {\n          label: e.label ?? \"\",\n          edgeType: e.edgeType ?? \"bezier\",\n          markerEnd: e.markerEnd ?? \"arrowclosed\",\n          markerStart: e.markerStart,\n          strokeWidth: e.strokeWidth,\n          strokeColor: e.strokeColor,\n          strokeStyle: e.strokeStyle,\n        },\n        style: isDeleted ? { opacity: 0.4 } : undefined,\n      } as FlowEdge);\n    }\n\n    return { displayNodes: nodes, displayEdges: edges };\n  }, [baseLayout, compareLayout, diffResult, filters]);\n\n  // Build overlay rects after initial render\n  useEffect(() => {\n    // Delay to allow React Flow to measure nodes\n    const timer = setTimeout(() => {\n      const rfNodes = getNodes();\n      const rects = buildOverlayRects(diffResult, filters, rfNodes, t);\n      setOverlayRects(rects);\n    }, 100);\n    return () => clearTimeout(timer);\n  }, [diffResult, filters, getNodes, t, displayNodes]);\n\n  // Flash animation when text panel item is clicked\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!flashTargetId) return;\n\n    let count = 0;\n    const interval = setInterval(() => {\n      count++;\n      if (count > FLASH_COUNT * 2) {\n        clearInterval(interval);\n        setFlashingIds(new Set());\n        return;\n      }\n      setFlashingIds(count % 2 === 1 ? new Set([flashTargetId]) : new Set());\n    }, FLASH_INTERVAL_MS);\n\n    return () => clearInterval(interval);\n  }, [flashTargetId]);\n\n  // Apply flash styling to nodes\n  const styledNodes = useMemo(() => {\n    return displayNodes.map((n) => {\n      const isFlashing = flashingIds.has(n.id);\n      if (isFlashing) {\n        return {\n          ...n,\n          style: {\n            ...n.style,\n            boxShadow: \"0 0 0 3px var(--color-primary)\",\n            transition: \"box-shadow 0.1s ease\",\n          },\n        };\n      }\n      return n;\n    });\n  }, [displayNodes, flashingIds]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"h-full w-full\">\n      <ReactFlow\n        nodes={styledNodes}\n        edges={displayEdges}\n        nodeTypes={nodeTypes}\n        edgeTypes={edgeTypes}\n        nodesDraggable={false}\n        nodesConnectable={false}\n        elementsSelectable={false}\n        connectOnClick={false}\n        zoomOnScroll\n        panOnDrag\n        fitView\n        proOptions={{ hideAttribution: true }}\n      >\n        <Panel position=\"top-left\">\n          <button\n            onClick={onExit}\n            className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-background/80 border border-border text-xs text-foreground backdrop-blur-sm hover:bg-accent cursor-pointer\"\n          >\n            <ArrowLeft size={14} />\n            {t(\"diffBack\")}\n          </button>\n        </Panel>\n\n        {/* Diff overlay rectangles */}\n        {overlayRects.map((rect, i) => {\n          const color = DIFF_COLORS[rect.kind];\n          return (\n            <Panel key={i} position=\"top-left\" style={{ margin: 0, padding: 0, pointerEvents: \"none\" }}>\n              <svg\n                style={{\n                  position: \"absolute\",\n                  left: 0,\n                  top: 0,\n                  width: \"100%\",\n                  height: \"100%\",\n                  pointerEvents: \"none\",\n                  overflow: \"visible\",\n                }}\n              >\n                {/* This will be positioned by React Flow's viewport transform */}\n              </svg>\n            </Panel>\n          );\n        })}\n      </ReactFlow>\n\n      {/* SVG overlay for diff rectangles — rendered as a sibling so it's in flow coordinates */}\n      <DiffOverlaySvg rects={overlayRects} t={t} />\n    </div>\n  );\n});\n\n/** SVG overlay that draws diff highlight rectangles in React Flow viewport space */\nfunction DiffOverlaySvg({ rects, t }: { rects: DiffOverlayRect[]; t: (key: string) => string }) {\n  // We need to render these within the React Flow viewport coordinate space\n  // This is done using a custom Panel with absolute positioning\n  // For now, we'll use a simpler approach with CSS positioned div overlays\n  return null; // Will be implemented with React Flow's viewport transform in next iteration\n}\n\ninterface DiffCanvasProps {\n  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  diffResult: DiffResult;\n  filters: DiffFilters;\n  flashTargetId: string | null;\n  onExit: () => void;\n}\n\nexport const DiffCanvas = memo(function DiffCanvas(props: DiffCanvasProps) {\n  return (\n    <ReactFlowProvider>\n      <DiffCanvasInner {...props} />\n    </ReactFlowProvider>\n  );\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/root/flowmaid/src/components/diffComparison/DiffFilterBar.tsx",
  "content": "\"use client\";\n\nimport { useLocale } from \"@/lib/i18n/useLocale\";\nimport type { DiffFilters } from \"@/lib/diff/types\";\n\ninterface DiffFilterBarProps {\n  filters: DiffFilters;\n  onFiltersChange: (filters: DiffFilters) => void;\n}\n\nexport function DiffFilterBar({ filters, onFiltersChange }: DiffFilterBarProps) {\n  const { t } = useLocale();\n\n  const toggle = (key: keyof DiffFilters) => {\n    onFiltersChange({ ...filters, [key]: !filters[key] });\n  };\n\n  const items: { key: keyof DiffFilters; label: string }[] = [\n    { key: \"edges\", label: t(\"diffFilterEdges\") },\n    { key: \"textChanges\", label: t(\"diffFilterText\") },\n    { key: \"positionChanges\", label: t(\"diffFilterPosition\") },\n    { key: \"styleChanges\", label: t(\"diffFilterStyle\") },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n      {items.map(({ key, label }) => (\n        <label key={key} className=\"flex items-center gap-1 text-xs cursor-pointer select-none\">\n          <input\n            type=\"checkbox\"\n            checked={filters[key]}\n            onChange={() => toggle(key)}\n            className=\"accent-primary h-3.5 w-3.5\"\n          />\n          {label}\n        </label>\n      ))}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}
