  "old_string": "interface DiffOverlayRect {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  width: number;\n  height: number;\n  kind: DiffKind;\n  label: string;\n  nodeIds: string[];\n}\n\n/** Padding around diff overlay rectangles */\nconst OVERLAY_PADDING = 12;\n\nfunction buildOverlayRects(\n  diffResult: DiffResult,\n  filters: DiffFilters,\n  nodes: Node[],\n  t: (key: TranslationKey) => string,\n): DiffOverlayRect[] {\n  const rects: DiffOverlayRect[] = [];\n  const nodeMap = new Map(nodes.map((n) => [n.id, n]));\n\n  // Collect individual diff items with positions\n  const items: { id: string; kind: DiffKind; x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }[] = [];\n\n  for (const nd of diffResult.nodeDiffs) {\n    if (nd.kind === \"unchanged\") continue;\n    if (nd.kind === \"moved\" && !filters.positionChanges) continue;\n    if (nd.kind === \"modified\") {\n      const hasVisibleChanges = nd.changedFields.some((f) => {\n        if ([\"label\", \"shape\"].includes(f)) return filters.textChanges;\n        if (f === \"position\") return filters.positionChanges;\n        return filters.styleChanges;\n      });\n      if (!hasVisibleChanges) continue;\n    }\n\n    const node = nodeMap.get(nd.nodeId);\n    if (!node) continue;\n\n    const styleW = typeof node.style?.width === \"number\" ? node.style.width : undefined;\n    const styleH = typeof node.style?.height === \"number\" ? node.style.height : undefined;\n    const w = (node.measured?.width ?? node.width ?? styleW ?? 150) as number;\n    const h = (node.measured?.height ?? node.height ?? styleH ?? 50) as number;\n\n    items.push({ id: nd.nodeId, kind: nd.kind, x: node.position.x, y: node.position.y, w, h });\n  }\n\n  // Group nearby items of the same kind\n  const used = new Set<number>();\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {\n    if (used.has(i)) continue;\n\n    const group = [items[i]];\n    used.add(i);\n\n    // Find nearby items of the same kind\n    for (let j = i + 1; j < items.length; j++) {\n      if (used.has(j)) continue;\n      if (items[j].kind !== items[i].kind) continue;\n\n      // Check if any item in the group is close enough\n      const isClose = group.some((g) => {\n        const dx = Math.abs((items[j].x + items[j].w / 2) - (g.x + g.w / 2));\n        const dy = Math.abs((items[j].y + items[j].h / 2) - (g.y + g.h / 2));\n        return dx < GROUPING_THRESHOLD + g.w / 2 + items[j].w / 2 &&\n               dy < GROUPING_THRESHOLD + g.h / 2 + items[j].h / 2;\n      });\n\n      if (isClose) {\n        group.push(items[j]);\n        used.add(j);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Compute bounding box\n    let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;\n    const nodeIds: string[] = [];\n    for (const item of group) {\n      minX = Math.min(minX, item.x);\n      minY = Math.min(minY, item.y);\n      maxX = Math.max(maxX, item.x + item.w);\n      maxY = Math.max(maxY, item.y + item.h);\n      nodeIds.push(item.id);\n    }\n\n    const kindLabels: Record<DiffKind, string> = {\n      added: t(\"diffKindAdded\"),\n      deleted: t(\"diffKindDeleted\"),\n      modified: t(\"diffKindModified\"),\n      moved: t(\"diffKindMoved\"),\n      unchanged: \"\",\n    };\n\n    rects.push({\n      x: minX - OVERLAY_PADDING,\n      y: minY - OVERLAY_PADDING,\n      width: maxX - minX + OVERLAY_PADDING * 2,\n      height: maxY - minY + OVERLAY_PADDING * 2,\n      kind: group[0].kind,\n      label: kindLabels[group[0].kind],\n      nodeIds,\n    });\n  }\n\n  return rects;\n}\n\nconst DIFF_COLORS: Record<DiffKind, string> = {\n  added: \"#22c55e\",\n  deleted: \"#ef4444\",\n  modified: \"#eab308\",\n  moved: \"#3b82f6\",\n  unchanged: \"transparent\",\n};\n\ninterface DiffCanvasInnerProps {",

全テスト通過。オニオンスキンモードの実装内容：

- **デフォルト（オニオンスキン）**: ベースと比較先を2層で重ね、下部中央のスライダーで透明度を調整。左にスライド＝ベースが濃く、右にスライド＝比較先が濃く表示
- **ファイル名Aボタン**: ベースのみ表示
- **ファイル名Bボタン**: 比較先のみ表示
- スライダーの両端にファイル名ラベル表示

    73→        strokeColor: e.strokeColor,
    74→        strokeStyle: e.strokeStyle,
    75→      },
    76→    } as FlowEdge);
    77→  }
    78→
    79→  return { displayNodes: nodes, displayEdges: edges };
    80→}
    81→
    82→const DiffCanvasInner = memo(function DiffCanvasInner({
    83→  baseLayout,
    84→  compareLayout,
    85→  onExit,
    86→  baseFileName,
    87→  compareFileName,
    88→}: DiffCanvasInnerProps) {
    89→  const { t } = useLocale();
    90→  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<DiffViewMode>("diff");
    91→  const [onionOpacity, setOnionOpacity] = useState(DEFAULT_ONION_OPACITY);
    92→
    93→  // Build nodes/edges for each layout
    94→  const baseData = useMemo(() => buildPlainNodesEdges(baseLayout), [baseLayout]);
    95→  const compareData = useMemo(() => buildPlainNodesEdges(compareLayout), [compareLayout]);
    96→
    97→  // Pick which data to show based on mode
    98→  const displayData = useMemo(() => {
    99→    if (viewMode === "base") return baseData;
   100→    if (viewMode === "compare") return compareData;
   101→    // diff mode: show base layer (compare layer is rendered as overlay)
   102→    return baseData;
   103→  }, [viewMode, baseData, compareData]);
   104→
   105→  const handleSliderChange = useCallback((e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
   106→    setOnionOpacity(Number(e.target.value) / ONION_SLIDER_STEPS);
   107→  }, []);
   108→
   109→  const isDiffMode = viewMode === "diff";
   110→
   111→  return (
   112→    <div className="h-full w-full relative">
   113→      {/* Base layer */}
   114→      <div
   115→        className="absolute inset-0"
   116→        style={{ opacity: isDiffMode ? 1 - onionOpacity : 1 }}
   117→      >
   118→        <ReactFlow
   119→          nodes={displayData.displayNodes}
   120→          edges={displayData.displayEdges}
   121→          nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
   122→          edgeTypes={edgeTypes}
   123→          nodesDraggable={false}
   124→          nodesConnectable={false}
   125→          elementsSelectable={false}
   126→          connectOnClick={false}
   127→          zoomOnScroll={!isDiffMode}
   128→          panOnDrag={!isDiffMode}
   129→          fitView
   130→          proOptions={{ hideAttribution: true }}
   131→        >
   132→          <Panel position="top-left">
   133→            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   134→              <button
   135→                onClick={onExit}
   136→                className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-background/80 border border-border text-xs text-foreground backdrop-blur-sm hover:bg-accent cursor-pointer"
   137→              >
   138→                <ArrowLeft size={14} />
   139→                {t("diffBack")}
   140→              </button>
   141→              <ToggleGroup
   142→                type="single"
   143→                value={viewMode}
   144→                onValueChange={(val) => { if (val) setViewMode(val as DiffViewMode); }}
   145→                className="bg-background/80 border border-border rounded-md backdrop-blur-sm"
   146→              >
   147→                <ToggleGroupItem value="diff" className="h-7 px-2.5 text-xs data-[state=on]:bg-accent">
   148→                  {t("diffViewDiff")}
   149→                </ToggleGroupItem>
   150→                <ToggleGroupItem value="base" className="h-7 px-2.5 text-xs data-[state=on]:bg-accent max-w-32 truncate">
   151→                  {baseFileName.replace(/\.flowmaid$/, "")}
   152→                </ToggleGroupItem>
   153→                <ToggleGroupItem value="compare" className="h-7 px-2.5 text-xs data-[state=on]:bg-accent max-w-32 truncate">
   154→                  {compareFileName.replace(/\.flowmaid$/, "")}
   155→                </ToggleGroupItem>
   156→              </ToggleGroup>
   157→            </div>
   158→          </Panel>
   159→
   160→          {/* Onion skin slider — bottom center */}
   161→          {isDiffMode && (
   162→            <Panel position="bottom-center">
   163→              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2 rounded-md bg-background/80 border border-border backdrop-blur-sm">
   164→                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground whitespace-nowrap">
   165→                  {baseFileName.replace(/\.flowmaid$/, "")}
   166→                </span>
   167→                <input
   168→                  type="range"
   169→                  min={0}
   170→                  max={ONION_SLIDER_STEPS}
   171→                  value={Math.round(onionOpacity * ONION_SLIDER_STEPS)}
   172→                  onChange={handleSliderChange}
   173→                  className="w-40 accent-primary"
   174→                />
   175→                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground whitespace-nowrap">
   176→                  {compareFileName.replace(/\.flowmaid$/, "")}
   177→                </span>
   178→              </div>
   179→            </Panel>
   180→          )}
   181→        </ReactFlow>
   182→      </div>
   183→
   184→      {/* Compare layer (onion skin overlay) — only in diff mode */}
   185→      {isDiffMode && (
   186→        <div
   187→          className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none"
   188→          style={{ opacity: onionOpacity }}
   189→        >
   190→          <ReactFlowProvider>
   191→            <ReactFlow
   192→              nodes={compareData.displayNodes}
   193→              edges={compareData.displayEdges}
   194→              nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
   195→              edgeTypes={edgeTypes}
   196→              nodesDraggable={false}
   197→              nodesConnectable={false}
   198→              elementsSelectable={false}
   199→              connectOnClick={false}
   200→              zoomOnScroll={false}
   201→              panOnDrag={false}
   202→              fitView
   203→              proOptions={{ hideAttribution: true }}
   204→            />
   205→          </ReactFlowProvider>
   206→        </div>
   207→      )}
   208→    </div>
   209→  );
   210→});
   211→
   212→interface DiffCanvasProps {
   213→  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
   214→  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
   215→  onExit: () => void;
   216→  baseFileName: string;
   217→  compareFileName: string;
   218→}
   219→
   220→export const DiffCanvas = memo(function DiffCanvas(props: DiffCanvasProps) {
   221→  return (
   222→    <ReactFlowProvider>
   223→      <DiffCanvasInner {...props} />
   224→    </ReactFlowProvider>
   225→  );
   226→});
   227→

   if (items[j].kind !== items[i].kind) continue;\n\n      const isClose = group.some((g) => {\n        const dx = Math.abs((items[j].x + items[j].w / 2) - (g.x + g.w / 2));\n        const dy = Math.abs((items[j].y + items[j].h / 2) - (g.y + g.h / 2));\n        return dx < GROUPING_THRESHOLD + g.w / 2 + items[j].w / 2 &&\n               dy < GROUPING_THRESHOLD + g.h / 2 + items[j].h / 2;\n string; seq: number } | null;\n  onExit: () => void;\n  baseFileName: string;\n  compareFileName: string;\n}\n\n/** Build plain nodes/edges from a single layout (no diff highlighting) */\nfunction buildPlainNodesEdges(layout: FlowmaidLayout): { displayNodes: FlowNode[]; displayEdges: FlowEdge[] } {\n  const nodes: FlowNode[] = [];\n  const edges: FlowEdge[] = [];\n\n  for (const [id, n] of id,\n      source: e.source,\n      target: e.target,\n      type: \"labeled\",\n      sourceHandle: e.sourceHandle,\n      targetHandle: e.targetHandle,\n      data: {\n        label: e.label ?? DiffCanvasInner({\n  baseLayout,\n  compareLayout,\n  diffResult,\n  filters,\n  flashTarget,\n  onExit,\n  baseFileName,\n  compareFileName,\n}: DiffCanvasInnerProps) {\n  const { t } = useLocale();\n  const [flashingIds, setFlashingIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());\n  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<DiffViewMode>(\"diff\");\n\n  // Set of moved node IDs (for semi-transparent old position)\n  const movedNodeIds = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!filters.positionChanges) return new Set<string>();\n    return new Set(\n      diffResult.nodeDiffs\n position: n.position,\n        data: {\n          label: n.label,\n          shape: n.shape as FlowNode[\"data\"][\"shape\"],\n          fillColor: n.fillColor,\n          borderColor: n.borderColor,\n        },\n        style: n.size ? { width: n.size.width, height: n.size.height } : undefined,\n      } as FlowNode);\n    }\n\n    // Add \"moved\" ghost nodes at compare position (new position)\n    for (const nd of diffResult.nodeDiffs) {\n      if (nd.kind !== \"moved\" || !nd.compareNode) continue;\n      if (!filters.positionChanges) continue;\n      const n = nd.compareNode;\n      nodes.push({\n        id: `${nd.nodeId}__moved`,\n        type: n.componentDefinitionId ? \"componentInstance\" : n.shape,\n        position: n.position,\n        data: {\n n.size.width, height: n.size.height } : undefined,\n      } as FlowNode);\n    }\n\n    // Build edges from base layout\n    for (const [id, e] of Object.entries(baseLayout.edges)) {\n      if (!filters.edges) continue;\n      const ed = diffResult.edgeDiffs.find((d) => d.edgeKey === id);\n      const isDeleted = ed?.kind === \"deleted\";\n\n      edges.push({\n        id,\n        source: e.source,\n        target: e.target,\n        type: \"labeled\",\n        sourceHandle: e.sourceHandle,\n        targetHandle: e.targetHandle,\n        data: {\n          label: e.label ?? \"\",\n          edgeType: e.edgeType ?? \"bezier\",\n          markerEnd: e.markerEnd ?? \"arrowclosed\",\n          markerStart: e.markerStart,\n          strokeWidth: e.strokeWidth,\n          strokeColor: layout (not in base)\n    if (filters.edges) {\n      const baseEdgeKeys = new Set(Object.keys(baseLayout.edges));\n      for (const [id, e] of Object.entries(compareLayout.edges)) {\n        if (baseEdgeKeys.has(id)) continue;\n        edges.push({\n          id,\n          source: e.source,\n          target: e.target,\n          type: \"labeled\",\n          sourceHandle: e.sourceHandle,\n          targetHandle: e.targetHandle,\n          data: {\n            label: e.label ?? \"\",\n            edgeType: e.edgeType ?? \"bezier\",\n            markerEnd: e.markerEnd ?? Set([flashTarget.id]) : new Set());\n    }, FLASH_INTERVAL_MS);\n\n    return () => clearInterval(interval);\n  }, [flashTarget]);\n\n  // Apply flash styling to nodes\n  const styledNodes = onClick={onExit}\n              className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-background/80 border border-border text-xs text-foreground backdrop-blur-sm hover:bg-accent cursor-pointer\"\n            >\n              <ArrowLeft size={14} />\n              {t(\"diffBack\")}\n            </button>\n            <ToggleGroup\n              type=\"single\"\n              value={viewMode}\n              onValueChange={(val) => { if (val) setViewMode(val as DiffViewMode); }}\n              className=\"bg-background/80 border border-border rounded-md backdrop-blur-sm\"\n            >\n              <ToggleGroupItem value=\"diff\" className=\"h-7 px-2.5 text-xs data-[state=on]:bg-accent\">\n                {t(\"diffViewDiff\")}\n              </ToggleGroupItem>\n              <ToggleGroupItem value=\"base\" className=\"h-7 px-2.5 text-xs data-[state=on]:bg-accent max-w-32 truncate\">\n                {baseFileName.replace(/\\.flowmaid$/, \"\")}\n              </ToggleGroupItem>\n              <ToggleGroupItem value=\"compare\" className=\"h-7 px-2.5 text-xs data-[state=on]:bg-accent max-w-32 truncate\">\n                {compareFileName.replace(/\\.flowmaid$/, \"\")}\n              </ToggleGroupItem>\n            </ToggleGroup>\n          </div>\n        </Panel>\n\n        {/* Diff overlay rendered inside ReactFlow for correct coordinate alignment */}\n        <DiffOverlay rects={overlayRects} />\n      </ReactFlow>\n    </div>\n  );\n});\n\n/** Renders diff highlight rectangles (dashed outlines + labels) in React Flow viewport space */\nfunction DiffOverlay({ rects }: { rects: DiffOverlayRect[] }) {\n  const { x, y, zoom } = useViewport();\n\n  if (rects.length === 0) return null;\n\n  const LABEL_FONT_SIZE = 11;\n  const LABEL_PADDING_X = 6;\n  const LABEL_PADDING_Y = 2;\n  const LABEL_OFFSET_Y = -8;\n\n  return (\n    <svg\n      style={{\n        position: \"absolute\",\n        top: 0,\n        left: 0,\n        width: \"100%\",\n        height: \"100%\",\n        pointerEvents: \"none\",\n        overflow: \"visible\",\n      }}\n    >\n      <g transform={`translate(${x},${y})             y={rect.y}\n                width={rect.width}\n                height={rect.height}\n                fill=\"none\"\n                stroke={color}\n                strokeWidth={2 / zoom}\n                strokeDasharray={`${6 / zoom} ${4 / zoom}`}\n                rx={4 / zoom}\n              />\n              <rect\n                x={rect.x}\n                y={rect.y + LABEL_OFFSET_Y / zoom - (LABEL_FONT_SIZE + LABEL_PADDING_Y * 2) / zoom}\n                width={(rect.label.length * LABEL_FONT_SIZE * 0.7 + LABEL_PADDING_X * 2) / zoom}\n                height={(LABEL_FONT_SIZE + LABEL_PADDING_Y * 2) / zoom}\n                fill={color}\n                rx={3 / zoom}\n              />\n              <text\n                x={rect.x + LABEL_PADDING_X / zoom}\n                y={rect.y + LABEL_OFFSET_Y / zoom - LABEL_PADDING_Y / zoom}\n                fill=\"white\"\n                fontSize={LABEL_FONT_SIZE / zoom}\n                fontWeight=\"bold\"\n                fontFamily=\"system-ui, sans-serif\"\n              >\n                {rect.label}\n              </text>\n            </g>\n          );\n        })}\n      </g>\n    </svg>\n  );\n}\n\ninterface DiffCanvasProps {\n  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  diffResult: DiffResult;\n  filters: DiffFilters;\n  flashTarget: { id: string; seq: number } | null;\n  onExit: () => void;\n  baseFileName: string;\n  compareFileName: string;\n}\n\nexport const DiffCanvas = memo(function DiffCanvas(props: DiffCanvasProps) {\n  return (\n    <ReactFlowProvider>\n      <DiffCanvasInner {...props} />\n    </ReactFlowProvider>\n  );\n});\n"
}

> DEVELOPER

これから徐々に修正していきます。差分表示画面で、右側のエッジが左側のエッジに繋がっているのですが
なぜですか？

> AGENT

差分モードでは、ベースのノード位置をベースにキャンバスを描画しています。比較先にのみ存在するエッジ（追加エッジ）は、`source`/`target` のノードIDがベース側のノード位置を参照してしまうため、意図しない接続になります。

例えば、比較先で追加されたエッジが `E → F` を接続する場合：
- ノード `E` が追加ノード（比較先の位置に表示） 
- ノード `F` がベースにも存在するノード（ベースの位置に表示）
- → エッジが比較先の `E` からベースの `F` に向かって描画され、不自然な線になる

修正方針として、**差分モードでは比較先のみのエッジを表示しない**（base/compare個別表示で確認する）のが最もシンプルです。対応します。

> TOOL

いま重なって表示してるじゃないですか？

<>ボタン押下で差分を入れ替えれるようにしたいと思うのですがどうですか？

> AGENT

面白いアイデアですね。変更があったノードの近くにポップオーバーのような小さいウィンドウが出て、`<` `>` ボタンでbefore/afterを切り替える方式ですか。

イメージの確認をさせてください：

```
通常時（合成ビュー）:
+------------------------------------------+
|                                          |
|    [A] ──→ [B] ──→ [D]                  |
|                     │                    |
|    ┌──────────┐     │                    |
|    │ < B >    │     ↓                    |
|    │ 開始→終了 │    [F]                   |
|    └──────────┘                          |
|                                          |
+------------------------------------------+

Bをクリックすると小窓が出現、<>で切替:
  < を押すとベース側の値（label: "開始"）
  > を押すと比較先の値（label: "終了"）
```

確認したい点：

1. **小窓の表示タイミング** — 変更のあるノードをクリックしたら出る？それとも変更ノード全部に常時表示？
2. **小窓の内容** — ノードのプレビュー（形状+ラベル）？テキスト情報（label, position等）？
3. **追加/削除ノード** — これらは小窓ではなく、通常通りの色分け表示（緑/赤半透明）のまま？
4. **ベースとなるキャンバス** — ベース側をベースに表示して、変更ノードだけ小窓で切替？

> DEVELOPER

イメージ画像作ってきました。
こんな感じで分かりますか？

- 差分がある箇所に赤点線の囲みが出る
- 囲みの右上に「ファイル名」のタブがあり、どちらのファイルの状態かを示す
- `< >` で切り替えると、その囲み内のノード/エッジがベース側⇔比較先側に入れ替わる

確認させてください：

1. **切り替えの単位** — 各差分囲みごとに個別に `< >` で切り替え？それとも全差分箇所を一括で切り替え？
2. **追加ノード** — 比較先にしかないノードは、`>` で比較先表示時にのみ見える？`<` でベース表示にすると消える？

- **追加ノード**: 緑点線囲み、切替なし（比較先の状態を常時表示）

    18→import { ToggleGroup, ToggleGroupItem } from "@/components/ui/toggle-group";
    19→import type { FlowNode, FlowEdge } from "@/store/types";
    20→
    21→type DiffViewMode = "diff" | "base" | "compare";
    22→
    23→/** Prefix for compare-side node/edge IDs to avoid conflicts with base-side IDs */
    24→const COMPARE_ID_PREFIX = "__cmp__";
    25→
    26→/** Distance threshold for grouping nearby changed nodes (px) */
    27→const GROUPING_THRESHOLD = 100;
    28→
    29→/** Number of times to flash a node when clicked in text panel */
    30→const FLASH_COUNT = 3;
    31→const FLASH_INTERVAL_MS = 200;
    32→
    33→interface DiffOverlayRect {
    34→  x: number;
    35→  y: number;
    36→  width: number;
    37→  height: number;
    38→  kind: DiffKind;
    39→  label: string;
    40→  nodeIds: string[];
    41→}
    42→
    43→/** Padding around diff overlay rectangles */
    44→const OVERLAY_PADDING = 12;
    45→
    46→function buildOverlayRects(
    47→  diffResult: DiffResult,
    48→  filters: DiffFilters,
    49→  nodes: Node[],
    50→  t: (key: TranslationKey) => string,
    51→): DiffOverlayRect[] {
    52→  const rects: DiffOverlayRect[] = [];
    53→  const nodeMap = new Map(nodes.map((n) => [n.id, n]));
    54→
    55→  const items: { id: string; kind: DiffKind; x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }[] = [];
    56→
    57→  for (const nd of diffResult.nodeDiffs) {
    58→    if (nd.kind === "unchanged") continue;
    59→    if (nd.kind === "moved" && !filters.positionChanges) continue;
    60→    if (nd.kind === "modified") {
    61→      const hasVisibleChanges = nd.changedFields.some((f) => {
    62→        if (["label", "shape"].includes(f)) return filters.textChanges;
    63→        if (f === "position") return filters.positionChanges;
    64→        return filters.styleChanges;
    65→      });
    66→      if (!hasVisibleChanges) continue;
    67→    }
    68→
    69→    const node = nodeMap.get(nd.nodeId);
    70→    if (!node) continue;
    71→
    72→    const styleW = typeof node.style?.width === "number" ? node.style.width : undefined;
    73→    const styleH = typeof node.style?.height === "number" ? node.style.height : undefined;
    74→    const w = (node.measured?.width ?? node.width ?? styleW ?? 150) as number;
    75→    const h = (node.measured?.height ?? node.height ?? styleH ?? 50) as number;
    76→
    77→    items.push({ id: nd.nodeId, kind: nd.kind, x: node.position.x, y: node.position.y, w, h });
    78→  }
    79→
    80→  const used = new Set<number>();
    81→
    82→  for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
    83→    if (used.has(i)) continue;
    84→
    85→    const group = [items[i]];
    86→    used.add(i);
    87→
    88→    for (let j = i + 1; j < items.length; j++) {
    89→      if (used.has(j)) continue;
    90→      if (items[j].kind !== items[i].kind) continue;
    91→
    92→      const isClose = group.some((g) => {
    93→        const dx = Math.abs((items[j].x + items[j].w / 2) - (g.x + g.w / 2));
    94→        const dy = Math.abs((items[j].y + items[j].h / 2) - (g.y + g.h / 2));
    95→        return dx < GROUPING_THRESHOLD + g.w / 2 + items[j].w / 2 &&
    96→               dy < GROUPING_THRESHOLD + g.h / 2 + items[j].h / 2;
    97→      });
    98→
    99→      if (isClose) {
   100→        group.push(items[j]);
   101→        used.add(j);
   102→      }
   103→    }
   104→
   105→    let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
   106→    const nodeIds: string[] = [];
   107→    for (const item of group) {
   108→      minX = Math.min(minX, item.x);
   109→      minY = Math.min(minY, item.y);
   110→      maxX = Math.max(maxX, item.x + item.w);
   111→      maxY = Math.max(maxY, item.y + item.h);
   112→      nodeIds.push(item.id);
   113→    }
   114→
   115→    const kindLabels: Record<DiffKind, string> = {
   116→      added: t("diffKindAdded"),
   117→      deleted: t("diffKindDeleted"),
   118→      modified: t("diffKindModified"),
   119→      moved: t("diffKindMoved"),
   120→      unchanged: "",
   121→    };
   122→
   123→    rects.push({
   124→      x: minX - OVERLAY_PADDING,
   125→      y: minY - OVERLAY_PADDING,
   126→      width: maxX - minX + OVERLAY_PADDING * 2,
   127→      height: maxY - minY + OVERLAY_PADDING * 2,
   128→      kind: group[0].kind,
   129→      label: kindLabels[group[0].kind],
   130→      nodeIds,
   131→    });
   132→  }
   133→
   134→  return rects;
   135→}
   136→
   137→const DIFF_COLORS: Record<DiffKind, string> = {
   138→  added: "#22c55e",
   139→  deleted: "#ef4444",
   140→  modified: "#eab308",
   141→  moved: "#3b82f6",
   142→  unchanged: "transparent",
   143→};
   144→
   145→interface DiffCanvasInnerProps {
   146→  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
   147→  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
   148→  diffResult: DiffResult;
   149→  filters: DiffFilters;
   150→  flashTarget: { id: string; seq: number } | null;
   151→  onExit: () => void;
   152→  baseFileName: string;
   153→  compareFileName: string;
   154→}
   155→
   156→/** Build plain nodes/edges from a single layout (no diff highlighting) */
   157→function buildPlainNodesEdges(layout: FlowmaidLayout): { displayNodes: FlowNode[]; displayEdges: FlowEdge[] } {
   158→  const nodes: FlowNode[] = [];
   159→  const edges: FlowEdge[] = [];
   160→
   161→  for (const [id, n] of Object.entries(layout.nodes)) {
   162→    nodes.push({
   163→      id,
   164→      type: n.componentDefinitionId ? "componentInstance" : n.shape,
   165→      position: n.position,
   166→      data: {
   167→        label: n.label,
   168→        shape: n.shape as FlowNode["data"]["shape"],
   169→        fillColor: n.fillColor,
   170→        borderColor: n.borderColor,
   171→        borderWidth: n.borderWidth,
   172→        borderStyle: n.borderStyle,
   173→        fontSize: n.fontSize,
   174→        textColor: n.textColor,
   175→        textAlign: n.textAlign,
   176→        bold: n.bold,
   177→        italic: n.italic,
   178→        underline: n.underline,
   179→      },
   180→      style: n.size ? { width: n.size.width, height: n.size.height } : undefined,
   181→    } as FlowNode);
   182→  }
   183→
   184→  for (const [id, e] of Object.entries(layout.edges)) {
   185→    edges.push({
   186→      id,
   187→      source: e.source,
   188→      target: e.target,
   189→      type: "labeled",
   190→      sourceHandle: e.sourceHandle,
   191→      targetHandle: e.targetHandle,
   192→      data: {
   193→        label: e.label ?? "",
   194→        edgeType: e.edgeType ?? "bezier",
   195→        markerEnd: e.markerEnd ?? "arrowclosed",
   196→        markerStart: e.markerStart,
   197→        strokeWidth: e.strokeWidth,
   198→        strokeColor: e.strokeColor,
   199→        strokeStyle: e.strokeStyle,
   200→      },
   201→    } as FlowEdge);
   202→  }
   203→
   204→  return { displayNodes: nodes, displayEdges: edges };
   205→}
   206→
   207→const DiffCanvasInner = memo(function DiffCanvasInner({
   208→  baseLayout,
   209→  compareLayout,
   210→  diffResult,
   211→  filters,
   212→  flashTarget,
   213→  onExit,
   214→  baseFileName,
   215→  compareFileName,
   216→}: DiffCanvasInnerProps) {
   217→  const { t } = useLocale();
   218→  const [flashingIds, setFlashingIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
   219→  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<DiffViewMode>("diff");
   220→
   221→  // Set of moved node IDs (for semi-transparent old position)
   222→  const movedNodeIds = useMemo(() => {
   223→    if (!filters.positionChanges) return new Set<string>();
   224→    return new Set(
   225→      diffResult.nodeDiffs
   226→        .filter((nd) => nd.kind === "moved" && nd.baseNode && nd.compareNode)
   227→        .map((nd) => nd.nodeId)
   228→    );
   229→  }, [diffResult, filters.positionChanges]);
   230→
   231→  // Convert layouts to React Flow nodes/edges for display
   232→  const { displayNodes, displayEdges } = useMemo(() => {
   233→    // Single-layout modes: show only base or compare without diff highlights
   234→    if (viewMode === "base") return buildPlainNodesEdges(baseLayout);
   235→    if (viewMode === "compare") return buildPlainNodesEdges(compareLayout);
   236→
   237→    const nodes: FlowNode[] = [];
   238→    const edges: FlowEdge[] = [];
   239→
   240→    // Build nodes from base layout
   241→    for (const [id, n] of Object.entries(baseLayout.nodes)) {
   242→      const nd = diffResult.nodeDiffs.find((d) => d.nodeId === id);
   243→      const isDeleted = nd?.kind === "deleted";
   244→      const isMoved = movedNodeIds.has(id);
   245→
   246→      nodes.push({
   247→        id,
   248→        type: n.componentDefinitionId ? "componentInstance" : n.shape,
   249→        position: n.position,
   250→        data: {
   251→          label: n.label,
   252→          shape: n.shape as FlowNode["data"]["shape"],
   253→          fillColor: n.fillColor,
   254→          borderColor: n.borderColor,
   255→          borderWidth: n.borderWidth,
   256→          borderStyle: n.borderStyle,
   257→          fontSize: n.fontSize,
   258→          textColor: n.textColor,
   259→          textAlign: n.textAlign,
   260→          bold: n.bold,
   261→          italic: n.italic,
   262→          underline: n.underline,
   263→        },
   264→        style: {
   265→          ...(n.size ? { width: n.size.width, height: n.size.height } : {}),
   266→          ...(isDeleted || isMoved ? { opacity: 0.4 } : {}),
   267→        },
   268→      } as FlowNode);
   269→    }
   270→
   271→    // Add compare-side nodes with prefixed IDs so compare edges connect independently
   272→    for (const [id, n] of Object.entries(compareLayout.nodes)) {
   273→      const nd = diffResult.nodeDiffs.find((d) => d.nodeId === id);
   274→      // Skip unchanged nodes (already shown via base)
   275→      if (nd?.kind === "unchanged") continue;
   276→
   277→      const isAdded = nd?.kind === "added";
   278→      const isMoved = nd?.kind === "moved";
   279→
   280→      // Only show compare-side node if it's added, moved, or modified
   281→      if (!isAdded && !isMoved && nd?.kind !== "modified") continue;
   282→
   283→      // For moved nodes, skip if position filter is off
   284→      if (isMoved && !filters.positionChanges) continue;
   285→
   286→      nodes.push({
   287→        id: `${COMPARE_ID_PREFIX}${id}`,
   288→        type: n.componentDefinitionId ? "componentInstance" : n.shape,
   289→        position: n.position,
   290→        data: {
   291→          label: n.label,
   292→          shape: n.shape as FlowNode["data"]["shape"],
   293→          fillColor: n.fillColor,
   294→          borderColor: n.borderColor,
   295→          borderWidth: n.borderWidth,
   296→          borderStyle: n.borderStyle,
   297→          fontSize: n.fontSize,
   298→          textColor: n.textColor,
   299→          textAlign: n.textAlign,
   300→          bold: n.bold,
   301→          italic: n.italic,
   302→          underline: n.underline,
   303→        },
   304→        style: {
   305→          ...(n.size ? { width: n.size.width, height: n.size.height } : {}),
   306→          // Modified nodes at compare position are hidden (base position shows the node)
   307→          ...(nd?.kind === "modified" ? { display: "none" as const } : {}),
   308→        },
   309→      } as FlowNode);
   310→    }
   311→
   312→    // Build edges from base layout
   313→    for (const [id, e] of Object.entries(baseLayout.edges)) {
   314→      if (!filters.edges) continue;
   315→      const ed = diffResult.edgeDiffs.find((d) => d.edgeKey === id);
   316→      const isDeleted = ed?.kind === "deleted";
   317→
   318→      edges.push({
   319→        id,
   320→        source: e.source,
   321→        target: e.target,
   322→        type: "labeled",
   323→        sourceHandle: e.sourceHandle,
   324→        targetHandle: e.targetHandle,
   325→        data: {
   326→          label: e.label ?? "",
   327→          edgeType: e.edgeType ?? "bezier",
   328→          markerEnd: e.markerEnd ?? "arrowclosed",
   329→          markerStart: e.markerStart,
   330→          strokeWidth: e.strokeWidth,
   331→          strokeColor: e.strokeColor,
   332→          strokeStyle: e.strokeStyle,
   333→        },
   334→        style: isDeleted ? { opacity: 0.4 } : undefined,
   335→      } as FlowEdge);
   336→    }
   337→
   338→    // Add compare-side edges with prefixed source/target IDs
   339→    if (filters.edges) {
   340→      const baseEdgeKeys = new Set(Object.keys(baseLayout.edges));
   341→      for (const [id, e] of Object.entries(compareLayout.edges)) {
   342→        if (baseEdgeKeys.has(id)) continue;
   343→
   344→        // Remap source/target to compare-side node IDs
   345→        const cmpSource = `${COMPARE_ID_PREFIX}${e.source}`;
   346→        const cmpTarget = `${COMPARE_ID_PREFIX}${e.target}`;
   347→
   348→        // Only add if both endpoints exist as compare-side nodes on canvas
   349→        const sourceExists = nodes.some((n) => n.id === cmpSource);
   350→        const targetExists = nodes.some((n) => n.id === cmpTarget);
   351→        if (!sourceExists || !targetExists) continue;
   352→
   353→        edges.push({
   354→          id: `${COMPARE_ID_PREFIX}${id}`,
   355→          source: cmpSource,
   356→          target: cmpTarget,
   357→          type: "labeled",
   358→          sourceHandle: e.sourceHandle,
   359→          targetHandle: e.targetHandle,
   360→          data: {
   361→            label: e.label ?? "",
   362→            edgeType: e.edgeType ?? "bezier",
   363→            markerEnd: e.markerEnd ?? "arrowclosed",
   364→            markerStart: e.markerStart,
   365→            strokeWidth: e.strokeWidth,
   366→            strokeColor: e.strokeColor,
   367→            strokeStyle: e.strokeStyle,
   368→          },
   369→        } as FlowEdge);
   370→      }
   371→    }
   372→
   373→    return { displayNodes: nodes, displayEdges: edges };
   374→  }, [baseLayout, compareLayout, diffResult, filters, movedNodeIds, viewMode]);
   375→
   376→  // Build overlay rects only in diff mode
   377→  const overlayRects = useMemo(
   378→    () => viewMode === "diff" ? buildOverlayRects(diffResult, filters, displayNodes, t) : [],
   379→    [diffResult, filters, displayNodes, t, viewMode]
   380→  );
   381→
   382→  // Flash animation when text panel item is clicked
   383→  useEffect(() => {
   384→    if (!flashTarget) return;
   385→
   386→    let count = 0;
   387→    const interval = setInterval(() => {
   388→      count++;
   389→      if (count > FLASH_COUNT * 2) {
   390→        clearInterval(interval);
   391→        setFlashingIds(new Set());
   392→        return;
   393→      }
   394→      setFlashingIds(count % 2 === 1 ? new Set([flashTarget.id]) : new Set());
   395→    }, FLASH_INTERVAL_MS);
   396→
   397→    return () => clearInterval(interval);
   398→  }, [flashTarget]);
   399→
   400→  // Apply flash styling to nodes
   401→  const styledNodes = useMemo(() => {
   402→    return displayNodes.map((n) => {
   403→      const isFlashing = flashingIds.has(n.id);
   404→      if (isFlashing) {
   405→        return {
   406→          ...n,
   407→          style: {
   408→            ...n.style,
   409→            boxShadow: "0 0 0 3px var(--color-primary)",
   410→            transition: "box-shadow 0.1s ease",
   411→          },
   412→        };
   413→      }
   414→      return n;
   415→    });
   416→  }, [displayNodes, flashingIds]);
   417→
   418→  return (
   419→    <div className="h-full w-full">
   420→      <ReactFlow
   421→        nodes={styledNodes}
   422→        edges={displayEdges}
   423→        nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
   424→        edgeTypes={edgeTypes}
   425→        nodesDraggable={false}
   426→        nodesConnectable={false}
   427→        elementsSelectable={false}
   428→        connectOnClick={false}
   429→        zoomOnScroll
   430→        panOnDrag
   431→        fitView
   432→        proOptions={{ hideAttribution: true }}
   433→      >
   434→        <Panel position="top-left">
   435→          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   436→            <button
   437→              onClick={onExit}
   438→              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md bg-background/80 border border-border text-xs text-foreground backdrop-blur-sm hover:bg-accent cursor-pointer"
   439→            >
   440→              <ArrowLeft size={14} />
   441→              {t("diffBack")}
   442→            </button>
   443→            <ToggleGroup
   444→              type="single"
   445→              value={viewMode}
   446→              onValueChange={(val) => { if (val) setViewMode(val as DiffViewMode); }}
   447→              className="bg-background/80 border border-border rounded-md backdrop-blur-sm"
   448→            >
   449→              <ToggleGroupItem value="diff" className="h-7 px-2.5 text-xs data-[state=on]:bg-accent">
   450→                {t("diffViewDiff")}
   451→              </ToggleGroupItem>
   452→              <ToggleGroupItem value="base" className="h-7 px-2.5 text-xs data-[state=on]:bg-accent max-w-32 truncate">
   453→                {baseFileName.replace(/\.flowmaid$/, "")}
   454→              </ToggleGroupItem>
   455→              <ToggleGroupItem value="compare" className="h-7 px-2.5 text-xs data-[state=on]:bg-accent max-w-32 truncate">
   456→                {compareFileName.replace(/\.flowmaid$/, "")}
   457→              </ToggleGroupItem>
   458→            </ToggleGroup>
   459→          </div>
   460→        </Panel>
   461→
   462→        {/* Diff overlay rendered inside ReactFlow for correct coordinate alignment */}
   463→        <DiffOverlay rects={overlayRects} />
   464→      </ReactFlow>
   465→    </div>
   466→  );
   467→});
   468→
   469→/** Renders diff highlight rectangles (dashed outlines + labels) in React Flow viewport space */
   470→function DiffOverlay({ rects }: { rects: DiffOverlayRect[] }) {
   471→  const { x, y, zoom } = useViewport();
   472→
   473→  if (rects.length === 0) return null;
   474→
   475→  const LABEL_FONT_SIZE = 11;
   476→  const LABEL_PADDING_X = 6;
   477→  const LABEL_PADDING_Y = 2;
   478→  const LABEL_OFFSET_Y = -8;
   479→
   480→  return (
   481→    <svg
   482→      style={{
   483→        position: "absolute",
   484→        top: 0,
   485→        left: 0,
   486→        width: "100%",
   487→        height: "100%",
   488→        pointerEvents: "none",
   489→        overflow: "visible",
   490→      }}
   491→    >
   492→      <g transform={`translate(${x},${y}) scale(${zoom})`}>
   493→        {rects.map((rect, i) => {
   494→          const color = DIFF_COLORS[rect.kind];
   495→          return (
   496→            <g key={i}>
   497→              <rect
   498→                x={rect.x}
   499→                y={rect.y}
   500→                width={rect.width}
   501→                height={rect.height}
   502→                fill="none"
   503→                stroke={color}
   504→                strokeWidth={2 / zoom}
   505→                strokeDasharray={`${6 / zoom} ${4 / zoom}`}
   506→                rx={4 / zoom}
   507→              />
   508→              <rect
   509→                x={rect.x}
   510→                y={rect.y + LABEL_OFFSET_Y / zoom - (LABEL_FONT_SIZE + LABEL_PADDING_Y * 2) / zoom}
   511→                width={(rect.label.length * LABEL_FONT_SIZE * 0.7 + LABEL_PADDING_X * 2) / zoom}
   512→                height={(LABEL_FONT_SIZE + LABEL_PADDING_Y * 2) / zoom}
   513→                fill={color}
   514→                rx={3 / zoom}
   515→              />
   516→              <text
   517→                x={rect.x + LABEL_PADDING_X / zoom}
   518→                y={rect.y + LABEL_OFFSET_Y / zoom - LABEL_PADDING_Y / zoom}
   519→                fill="white"
   520→                fontSize={LABEL_FONT_SIZE / zoom}
   521→                fontWeight="bold"
   522→                fontFamily="system-ui, sans-serif"
   523→              >
   524→                {rect.label}
   525→              </text>
   526→            </g>
   527→          );
   528→        })}
   529→      </g>
   530→    </svg>
   531→  );
   532→}
   533→
   534→interface DiffCanvasProps {
   535→  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
   536→  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
   537→  diffResult: DiffResult;
   538→  filters: DiffFilters;
   539→  flashTarget: { id: string; seq: number } | null;
   540→  onExit: () => void;
   541→  baseFileName: string;
   542→  compareFileName: string;
   543→}
   544→
   545→export const DiffCanvas = memo(function DiffCanvas(props: DiffCanvasProps) {
   546→  return (
   547→    <ReactFlowProvider>
   548→      <DiffCanvasInner {...props} />
   549→    </ReactFlowProvider>
   550→  );
   551→});
   552→

with positions for grouping\n  const items: { id: string; kind: DiffKind; x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }[] = [];\n\n  for (const nd of diffResult.nodeDiffs) {\n    if (nd.kind === const w = nodeLayout.size?.width ?? 150;\n    const h = nodeLayout.size?.height ?? 50;\n\n    items.push({ id: nd.nodeId, kind: nd.kind, x: nodeLayout.position.x, y: nodeLayout.position.y, w, h });\n used.add(i);\n\n    for (let j = i + 1; j < items.length; j++) {\n      if (used.has(j)) continue;\n      if (items[j].kind !== items[i].kind) continue;\n\n      const isClose = group.some((g) => {\n        const dx = Math.abs((items[j].x + items[j].w / 2) - (g.x + g.w / 2));\n        const dy = Math.abs((items[j].y + items[j].h / 2) - (g.y + g.h / 2));\n        return dx < GROUPING_THRESHOLD + g.w / 2 + items[j].w / 2 &&\n               dy < GROUPING_THRESHOLD + g.h / 2 + items[j].h / 2;\n      });\n\n      if (isClose) {\n        group.push(items[j]);\n        used.add(j);\n      }\n    }\n\n and compare\n  const toggleGroup = useCallback((groupKey: string) => {\n    setGroupStates((prev) => ({\n      ...prev,\n      [groupKey]: prev[groupKey] === \"compare\" ? \"base\" : \"compare\",\n to\n    const nodeToGroup = new Map<string, DiffGroup>();\n    for (const group of diffGroups) {\n      for (const nid of group.nodeIds) {\n        nodeToGroup.set(nid, group);\n      }\n    }\n\n for (const id of allNodeIds) {\n      const nd = diffResult.nodeDiffs.find((d) => d.nodeId === id);\n      const kind = nd?.kind ?? \"unchanged\";\n      const baseNode = baseLayout.nodes[id];\n <div\n              className=\"flex items-center gap-0 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold select-none\"\n              style={{ backgroundColor: color }}\n            >\n              {/* Label */}\n </>\n  );\n}\n\n// ─── Outer wrapper ───\n\ninterface DiffCanvasProps {\n  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  diffResult: DiffResult;\n  filters: DiffFilters;\n 全テスト通過。実装内容：

### 合成ビュー + グループ切替

**キャンバス表示:**
- **未変更ノード**: そのまま表示
- **追加ノード**: 緑点線囲み + ラベル（切替なし）
- **削除ノード**: 赤点線囲み + 半透明表示（切替なし）
- **変更/移動ノード**: 点線囲み + `< ファイル名 >` トグル付き
  - デフォルトはベース側を表示
  - `>` でcompare側に切替、`<` でbase側に戻る
  - 近接した同種の変更は自動グルーピング

**トグルコントロール:**
- 囲みの上にHTML要素で配置（色付きバッジ）
- `変更 < ファイル名 >` の形式
- `<` `>` ボタンでbase/compareを切替
- 切替時にノードの内容（ラベル、位置、スタイル）が入れ替わる

なんの変更は検知するようにしていたんでしたっけ？

> AGENT

仕様書と`computeDiff.ts`に基づくと、以下を検知しています：

### ノードの差分

| 差分種別 | 検知条件 |
|---------|---------|
| **追加** | 比較先にのみ存在 |
| **削除** | ベースにのみ存在 |
| **移動** | position のみ異なる（他プロパティ同一） |
| **変更** | label, shape, スタイル（色・枠線・フォント等）のいずれかが異なる |

### エッジの差分

| 差分種別 | 検知条件 |
|---------|---------|
| **追加** | 比較先にのみ存在 |
| **削除** | ベースにのみ存在 |
| **変更** | label, edgeType, スタイル（色・太さ・線種等）が異なる |

### コンポーネント定義の差分

| 差分種別 | 検知条件 |
|---------|---------|
| **追加/削除** | 定義IDが片方にのみ存在 |
| **変更** | name, 内部ノード/エッジ, entryNodeId, exitNodeId, direction が異なる |

### フィルタで表示/非表示を制御

ツールバーの4つのチェックボックス：
- **エッジの差分** — エッジの追加/削除/変更
- **テキストの差分** — label, shape の変更
- **位置移動の差分** — position の変更（移動ノード）
- **スタイル変更の差分** — 色・枠線・フォント等のスタイル変更

**検知対象の変更:**
- ノード: 追加/削除/変更（labelのみ）。移動は検知しない
- エッジ: 追加/削除のみ。変更は検知しない

**フィルタの変更:**

     1→import type { FlowmaidLayout, FlowmaidNodeLayout, FlowmaidEdgeLayout } from "@/lib/flowmaid/schema";
     2→import type { ComponentDefinition } from "@/types/flow";
     3→import type { DiffResult, NodeDiff, EdgeDiff, ComponentDefDiff, DiffKind } from "./types";
     4→
     5→/** Fields that are considered "text" changes */
     6→const TEXT_FIELDS: (keyof FlowmaidNodeLayout)[] = ["label", "shape"];
     7→
     8→/** Fields that are considered "position" changes */
     9→const POSITION_FIELDS: (keyof FlowmaidNodeLayout)[] = ["position"];
    10→
    11→/** Fields that are considered "style" changes for nodes */
    12→const NODE_STYLE_FIELDS: (keyof FlowmaidNodeLayout)[] = [
    13→  "fillColor", "fillOpacity", "fillLightness",
    14→  "borderColor", "borderOpacity", "borderLightness",
    15→  "borderWidth", "borderStyle",
    16→  "fontSize", "textColor", "textOpacity", "textLightness",
    17→  "textAlign", "textVerticalAlign",
    18→  "bold", "italic", "underline",
    19→  "size", "zIndex",
    20→];
    21→
    22→/** Fields that are considered "text" changes for edges */
    23→const EDGE_TEXT_FIELDS: (keyof FlowmaidEdgeLayout)[] = ["label"];
    24→
    25→/** Fields that are considered "style" changes for edges */
    26→const EDGE_STYLE_FIELDS: (keyof FlowmaidEdgeLayout)[] = [
    27→  "edgeType", "markerStart", "markerEnd",
    28→  "strokeWidth", "strokeColor", "strokeOpacity", "strokeLightness",
    29→  "strokeStyle",
    30→];
    31→
    32→function deepEqual(a: unknown, b: unknown): boolean {
    33→  if (a === b) return true;
    34→  if (a == null || b == null) return a == b;
    35→  if (typeof a !== typeof b) return false;
    36→  if (typeof a !== "object") return false;
    37→  const keysA = Object.keys(a as Record<string, unknown>);
    38→  const keysB = Object.keys(b as Record<string, unknown>);
    39→  if (keysA.length !== keysB.length) return false;
    40→  for (const key of keysA) {
    41→    if (!deepEqual((a as Record<string, unknown>)[key], (b as Record<string, unknown>)[key])) {
    42→      return false;
    43→    }
    44→  }
    45→  return true;
    46→}
    47→
    48→function getChangedFields<T extends object>(
    49→  base: T,
    50→  compare: T,
    51→  fields: (keyof T)[]
    52→): string[] {
    53→  const changed: string[] = [];
    54→  for (const field of fields) {
    55→    if (!deepEqual(base[field], compare[field])) {
    56→      changed.push(field as string);
    57→    }
    58→  }
    59→  return changed;
    60→}
    61→
    62→function computeNodeDiffs(base: FlowmaidLayout, compare: FlowmaidLayout): NodeDiff[] {
    63→  const diffs: NodeDiff[] = [];
    64→  const baseNodes = base.nodes;
    65→  const compareNodes = compare.nodes;
    66→
    67→  const allIds = new Set([...Object.keys(baseNodes), ...Object.keys(compareNodes)]);
    68→
    69→  for (const id of allIds) {
    70→    const baseNode = baseNodes[id];
    71→    const compareNode = compareNodes[id];
    72→
    73→    if (!baseNode) {
    74→      diffs.push({ kind: "added", nodeId: id, compareNode, changedFields: [] });
    75→      continue;
    76→    }
    77→    if (!compareNode) {
    78→      diffs.push({ kind: "deleted", nodeId: id, baseNode, changedFields: [] });
    79→      continue;
    80→    }
    81→
    82→    const textChanged = getChangedFields(baseNode, compareNode, TEXT_FIELDS);
    83→    const posChanged = getChangedFields(baseNode, compareNode, POSITION_FIELDS);
    84→    const styleChanged = getChangedFields(baseNode, compareNode, NODE_STYLE_FIELDS);
    85→
    86→    const allChanged = [...textChanged, ...posChanged, ...styleChanged];
    87→
    88→    if (allChanged.length === 0) {
    89→      diffs.push({ kind: "unchanged", nodeId: id, baseNode, compareNode, changedFields: [] });
    90→    } else if (posChanged.length > 0 && textChanged.length === 0 && styleChanged.length === 0) {
    91→      diffs.push({ kind: "moved", nodeId: id, baseNode, compareNode, changedFields: allChanged });
    92→    } else {
    93→      diffs.push({ kind: "modified", nodeId: id, baseNode, compareNode, changedFields: allChanged });
    94→    }
    95→  }
    96→
    97→  return diffs;
    98→}
    99→
   100→function computeEdgeDiffs(base: FlowmaidLayout, compare: FlowmaidLayout): EdgeDiff[] {
   101→  const diffs: EdgeDiff[] = [];
   102→  const baseEdges = base.edges;
   103→  const compareEdges = compare.edges;
   104→
   105→  const allKeys = new Set([...Object.keys(baseEdges), ...Object.keys(compareEdges)]);
   106→
   107→  for (const key of allKeys) {
   108→    const baseEdge = baseEdges[key];
   109→    const compareEdge = compareEdges[key];
   110→
   111→    if (!baseEdge) {
   112→      diffs.push({ kind: "added", edgeKey: key, compareEdge, changedFields: [] });
   113→      continue;
   114→    }
   115→    if (!compareEdge) {
   116→      diffs.push({ kind: "deleted", edgeKey: key, baseEdge, changedFields: [] });
   117→      continue;
   118→    }
   119→
   120→    const textChanged = getChangedFields(baseEdge, compareEdge, EDGE_TEXT_FIELDS);
   121→    const styleChanged = getChangedFields(baseEdge, compareEdge, EDGE_STYLE_FIELDS);
   122→
   123→    const allChanged = [...textChanged, ...styleChanged];
   124→
   125→    if (allChanged.length === 0) {
   126→      diffs.push({ kind: "unchanged", edgeKey: key, baseEdge, compareEdge, changedFields: [] });
   127→    } else {
   128→      diffs.push({ kind: "modified", edgeKey: key, baseEdge, compareEdge, changedFields: allChanged });
   129→    }
   130→  }
   131→
   132→  return diffs;
   133→}
   134→
   135→function computeComponentDefDiffs(
   136→  baseDefs: ComponentDefinition[],
   137→  compareDefs: ComponentDefinition[]
   138→): ComponentDefDiff[] {
   139→  const diffs: ComponentDefDiff[] = [];
   140→  const baseMap = new Map(baseDefs.map((d) => [d.id, d]));
   141→  const compareMap = new Map(compareDefs.map((d) => [d.id, d]));
   142→
   143→  const allIds = new Set([...baseMap.keys(), ...compareMap.keys()]);
   144→
   145→  for (const id of allIds) {
   146→    const baseDef = baseMap.get(id);
   147→    const compareDef = compareMap.get(id);
   148→
   149→    if (!baseDef) {
   150→      diffs.push({ kind: "added", defId: id, compareDef, changedFields: [] });
   151→      continue;
   152→    }
   153→    if (!compareDef) {
   154→      diffs.push({ kind: "deleted", defId: id, baseDef, changedFields: [] });
   155→      continue;
   156→    }
   157→
   158→    const changedFields: string[] = [];
   159→    if (baseDef.name !== compareDef.name) changedFields.push("name");
   160→    if (baseDef.entryNodeId !== compareDef.entryNodeId) changedFields.push("entryNodeId");
   161→    if (baseDef.exitNodeId !== compareDef.exitNodeId) changedFields.push("exitNodeId");
   162→    if (!deepEqual(baseDef.nodes, compareDef.nodes)) changedFields.push("nodes");
   163→    if (!deepEqual(baseDef.edges, compareDef.edges)) changedFields.push("edges");
   164→    if (baseDef.direction !== compareDef.direction) changedFields.push("direction");
   165→
   166→    if (changedFields.length === 0) {
   167→      diffs.push({ kind: "unchanged", defId: id, baseDef, compareDef, changedFields: [] });
   168→    } else {
   169→      diffs.push({ kind: "modified", defId: id, baseDef, compareDef, changedFields });
   170→    }
   171→  }
   172→
   173→  return diffs;
   174→}
   175→
   176→/**
   177→ * Compute differences between two .flowmaid layouts.
   178→ * Pure function — no side effects or external state dependencies.
   179→ */
   180→export function computeDiff(base: FlowmaidLayout, compare: FlowmaidLayout): DiffResult {
   181→  return {
   182→    nodeDiffs: computeNodeDiffs(base, compare),
   183→    edgeDiffs: computeEdgeDiffs(base, compare),
   184→    componentDefDiffs: computeComponentDefDiffs(
   185→      base.componentDefinitions ?? [],
   186→      compare.componentDefinitions ?? []
   187→    ),
   188→  };
   189→}
   190→
```

  "new_string": "function computeNodeDiffs(base: FlowmaidLayout, compare: FlowmaidLayout): NodeDiff[] {\n  const diffs: NodeDiff[] = [];\n  const baseNodes = base.nodes;\n  const compareNodes = compare.nodes;\n\n  const allIds = new Set([...Object.keys(baseNodes), ...Object.keys(compareNodes)]);\n\n  for (const id of allIds) {\n    const baseNode = baseNodes[id];\n    const compareNode = compareNodes[id];\n\n    if (!baseNode) {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"added\", nodeId: id, compareNode, changedFields: [] });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (!compareNode) {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"deleted\", nodeId: id, baseNode, changedFields: [] });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    // Only detect label changes for \"modified\"\n    const changedFields: string[] = [];\n    if (baseNode.label !== compareNode.label) changedFields.push(\"label\");\n\n    if (changedFields.length === 0) {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"unchanged\", nodeId: id, baseNode, compareNode, changedFields: [] });\n    } else {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"modified\", nodeId: id, baseNode, compareNode, changedFields });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return diffs;\n}"
}

  "new_string": "function computeEdgeDiffs(base: FlowmaidLayout, compare: FlowmaidLayout): EdgeDiff[] {\n  const diffs: EdgeDiff[] = [];\n  const baseEdges = base.edges;\n  const compareEdges = compare.edges;\n\n  const allKeys = new Set([...Object.keys(baseEdges), ...Object.keys(compareEdges)]);\n\n  for (const key of allKeys) {\n    const baseEdge = baseEdges[key];\n    const compareEdge = compareEdges[key];\n\n    if (!baseEdge) {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"added\", edgeKey: key, compareEdge, changedFields: [] });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (!compareEdge) {\n      diffs.push({ kind: \"deleted\", edgeKey: key, baseEdge, changedFields: [] });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    // Edges: detect added/deleted only, no modification detection\n    diffs.push({ kind: \"unchanged\", edgeKey: key, baseEdge, compareEdge, changedFields: [] });\n  }\n\n  return diffs;\n}"
}

    82→function buildDiffGroups(
    83→  diffResult: DiffResult,
    84→  filters: DiffFilters,
    85→  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout,
    86→  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout,
    87→  t: (key: TranslationKey) => string,
    88→): DiffGroup[] {
    89→  const groups: DiffGroup[] = [];
    90→
    91→  // Collect items with positions for grouping
    92→  const items: { id: string; kind: DiffKind; x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }[] = [];
    93→
    94→  for (const nd of diffResult.nodeDiffs) {
    95→    if (nd.kind === "unchanged") continue;
    96→    if (nd.kind === "moved" && !filters.positionChanges) continue;
    97→    if (nd.kind === "modified") {
    98→      const hasVisibleChanges = nd.changedFields.some((f) => {
    99→        if (["label", "shape"].includes(f)) return filters.textChanges;
   100→        if (f === "position") return filters.positionChanges;
   101→        return filters.styleChanges;
   102→      });
   103→      if (!hasVisibleChanges) continue;
   104→    }
   105→
   106→    // Use base position if available, otherwise compare position
   107→    const nodeLayout = nd.baseNode ?? nd.compareNode;
   108→    if (!nodeLayout) continue;
   109→
   110→    const w = nodeLayout.size?.width ?? 150;
   111→    const h = nodeLayout.size?.height ?? 50;
   112→
   113→    items.push({ id: nd.nodeId, kind: nd.kind, x: nodeLayout.position.x, y: nodeLayout.position.y, w, h });
   114→  }
   115→
   116→  // Group nearby items of the same kind
   117→  const used = new Set<number>();
   118→
   119→  for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
   120→    if (used.has(i)) continue;
   121→
   122→    const group = [items[i]];
   123→    used.add(i);
   124→
   125→    for (let j = i + 1; j < items.length; j++) {
   126→      if (used.has(j)) continue;
   127→      if (items[j].kind !== items[i].kind) continue;
   128→
   129→      const isClose = group.some((g) => {
   130→        const dx = Math.abs((items[j].x + items[j].w / 2) - (g.x + g.w / 2));
   131→        const dy = Math.abs((items[j].y + items[j].h / 2) - (g.y + g.h / 2));
   132→        return dx < GROUPING_THRESHOLD + g.w / 2 + items[j].w / 2 &&
   133→               dy < GROUPING_THRESHOLD + g.h / 2 + items[j].h / 2;
   134→      });
   135→
   136→      if (isClose) {
   137→        group.push(items[j]);
   138→        used.add(j);
   139→      }

   239→  // Toggle a group's view between base and compare

   241→    setGroupStates((prev) => ({

   243→      [groupKey]: prev[groupKey] === "compare" ? "base" : "compare",
   244→    }));
   245→  }, []);

   259→        nodeToGroup.set(nid, group);
   260→      }

   273→      const compareNode = compareLayout.nodes[id];
   274→
   275→      if (kind === "unchanged" && baseNode) {
   276→        // Unchanged: show base version
   277→        nodes.push(buildNodeFromLayout(id, baseNode));
   278→        continue;
   279→      }
   280→
   281→      if (kind === "added" && compareNode) {
   282→        // Added: always show compare version
   283→        nodes.push(buildNodeFromLayout(id, compareNode));
   284→        continue;
   285→      }
   286→
   287→      if (kind === "deleted" && baseNode) {
   288→        // Deleted: always show base version, semi-transparent

   293→      if ((kind === "modified" || kind === "moved") && baseNode && compareNode) {

## 調査報告：Flowmaid Diff機能の現在実装状況

### 1. **DiffCanvas構成** (`src/components/diffComparison/DiffCanvas.tsx`)

#### 全体構造
- **ReactFlowProvider でラップ**: DiffCanvasInner を外側で ReactFlowProvider でラップ（独立したReactFlowインスタンス）
- **表示モード**: 3種類のビューを `DiffViewMode` で管理
  - `"diff"`: マージされたビュー（両ファイルを重ねて表示、差分ハイライト付き）
  - `"base"`: ベースファイルのみ表示
  - `"compare"`: 比較ファイルのみ表示

#### Props（DiffCanvasInnerProps）
```typescript
interface DiffCanvasInnerProps {
  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
  diffResult: DiffResult;
  filters: DiffFilters;
  flashTarget: { id: string; seq: number } | null;
  onExit: () => void;
  baseFileName: string;
  compareFileName: string;
}
```

#### 主要ロジック

**ノード/エッジ構築**（`buildNodeFromLayout`）:
- FlowmaidLayout → FlowNode への変換関数
- オプションで追加 CSSプロパティ（opacity など）を上書き

**Diff Groups生成**（`buildDiffGroups`）:
- diffResult から変更箇所をグループ化（GROUPING_THRESHOLD=100px で近い変更をまとめる）
- 各グループは kind（added/deleted/modified）、位置、サイズを保持
- modified グループのみ toggleable=true（base/compare を切り替え可能）

**マージノード/エッジ生成**（useMemo内）:
- viewMode="diff" 時: 両ファイルを統合
  - unchanged: base版を表示
  - added: compare版を表示（filters.addedで制御）
  - deleted: base版を表示（opacity=0.4）
  - modified: グループ状態でbase/compare切り替え可能
- viewMode="base"|"compare": 単一ファイル表示

**DiffOverlay**:
- `useViewport()` で現在のズーム・パン状態を取得
- SVG dashed rectangles（zoom/translate で正規化）を描画
- toggleable グループの上に HTML制御ボタン（< > ボタン）をオーバーレイ
- スクリーンスペース位置を計算: `screenX = group.x * zoom + x`

#### React Flow設定
- `nodesDraggable={false}`, `nodesConnectable={false}`, `elementsSelectable={false}` → 読み取り専用
- `zoomOnScroll`, `panOnDrag`, `fitView` 有効
- Panel: top-left に戻るボタン + viewMode切り替え ToggleGroup

---

### 2. **BulkEditCanvasのパン/ズーム管理** (`src/components/bulkEdit/BulkEditCanvas.tsx`)

#### 構造
- **独立ReactFlowProvider**: BulkEditCanvasInner を ReactFlowProvider でラップ

#### Props（BulkEditCanvasInnerProps）
```typescript
interface BulkEditCanvasInnerProps {
  focusTarget: { type: "node" | "edge"; id: string } | null;
  highlightId: string | null;
  onNodeClick?: (nodeId: string) => void;
  onEdgeClick?: (edgeId: string) => void;
  onSelectionChange?: (selectedIds: string[]) => string[]; // 複数選択IDs
  onPaneClick?: () => void;
  onExit?: () => void;
}
```

#### ズーム/フォーカス管理

**useReactFlow フック**:
```typescript
const { fitBounds, getNodes, getEdges } = useReactFlow();
```

**フォーカス時の自動ズーム**（useEffect内）:
- `focusTarget` が変わると `fitBounds()` で指定要素をズーム＆フォーカス
- **ノード**: `fitBounds({ x, y, width, height }, { padding: 1.0, duration: 300 })`
- **エッジ**: ソース・ターゲットの両ノードを包含する矩形で fitBounds

**パン設定**:
```typescript
panOnDrag={PAN_ON_DRAG_BUTTONS}  // [1, 2] = middle + right mouse
selectionOnDrag  // 左ドラッグで範囲選択
```

**選択管理**:
- `useCtrlSelection` で統一的な選択ロジック（Ctrl+click, Ctrl+drag XOR）
- `localSelectedIds` ローカルステート
- multi-selection 時は selectedIds 配列を返す

---

### 3. **ReactFlow ビューポート同期方法**

現在の実装では **複数ReactFlowインスタンス間のビューポート同期は実装されていません**。

#### useViewportの使用例（DiffCanvas）
```typescript
const { x, y, zoom } = useViewport();
// SVG変換用: translate(${x},${y}) scale(${zoom})
```

#### 制約
- **Loose Coupling**: DiffCanvas と DiffTextPanel は独立
- **一方向通信**: DiffTextPanel → 特定ノード/エッジをクリック → DiffCanvas の flashTarget を更新（点滅アニメーション）
- **ビューポート同期なし**: DiffCanvas でズームしても DiffTextPanel の列幅は固定

#### 利用可能なAPI（@xyflow/react）
```typescript
// ビューポート取得
const { x, y, zoom } = useViewport();

// ビューポート設定
const { setViewport } = useReactFlow();
setViewport({ x, y, zoom }, { duration: 300 });

// ビューポート変更監視
<ReactFlow onViewportChange={(viewport) => { ... }}>
```

**サイドバイサイド再構築時の注意**:
- DiffCanvasLeft と DiffCanvasRight に分割した場合、各自に独立した ReactFlowProvider が必要
- 左右同期ズームには `onViewportChange` を外側の状態管理で統合

---

### 4. **EditorLayout の Diff モード統合** (`src/components/layout/EditorLayout.tsx`)

#### 構造
```
EditorLayout
└─ ReactFlowProvider（メイン。外側）
   └─ DnDProvider
      ├─ isBulkEditMode=true
      │  └─ ResizablePanelGroup（horizontal）
      │     ├─ BulkEditCanvas（独立Provider内）
      │     └─ BulkEditTable
      │
      ├─ isDiffMode=true && diffStep="compare"
      │  └─ ResizablePanelGroup（horizontal）
      │     ├─ DiffCanvas（独立Provider内）
      │     └─ DiffTextPanel
      │
      └─ 通常モード
         ├─ CollapsiblePanel（left: NodePalette）
         └─ ResizablePanelGroup（horizontal）
            ├─ FlowCanvas（EditorLayout外のProvider内）
            └─ MermaidPreview
```

#### Diff State管理
```typescript
const [isDiffMode, setIsDiffMode] = useState(false);
const [diffStep, setDiffStep] = useState<"import" | "compare">("import");
const [diffBaseLayout, setDiffBaseLayout] = useState<FlowmaidLayout | null>(null);
const [diffCompareLayout, setDiffCompareLayout] = useState<FlowmaidLayout | null>(null);
const [diffResult, setDiffResult] = useState<DiffResult | null>(null);
const [diffFilters, setDiffFilters] = useState<DiffFilters>(DEFAULT_DIFF_FILTERS);
const [diffFlashTarget, setDiffFlashTarget] = useState<{ id: string; seq: number } | null>(null);
```

#### フロー
1. **import ステップ**: DiffImportPanel でベース・比較ファイル選択
2. **compare ステップ**: DiffCanvas + DiffTextPanel でサイドバイサイド比較
3. **テキストパネルクリック**: `handleDiffTextItemClick` → `diffFlashTarget` 更新 → DiffCanvas 内でノードが点滅

---

### 5. **ComponentEditingHeader関連** (`src/components/flowComponent/ComponentEditingHeader.tsx`)

#### 役割
- コンポーネント編集モード中のヘッダー表示（メインキャンバス上部）
- Entry→Exit 接続確認と保存/破棄処理

#### 統合ポイント
- EditorLayout 内で `isEditingComponent` により表示/非表示を制御
- padding/background でテーマ反転フレーム効果

---

## まとめ：サイドバイサイド再構築の課題

| 項目 | 現状 | 再構築時の課題 |
|------|------|---------------|
| **Diff UI レイアウト** | 左: DiffCanvas、右: DiffTextPanel | 複数パネルの分割レイアウト対応、リサイザー配置 |
| **ビューポート同期** | 未実装（独立） | DiffCanvasLeft/Right のズーム・パンを同期 |
| **フォーカス/フラッシュ** | テキストパネルクリック → DiffCanvas 単一パネル対応 | 左右のどちらに表示するか決定ロジック必要 |
| **ReactFlowProvider 構造** | メインの外側で独立Provider | 同期が必要な場合、外側でまとめるか検討 |
| **フィルター UI** | Toolbar に統合 | 両パネルに共通適用でOK（現状と同じ） |
| **戻るボタン** | DiffCanvas に Panelで配置 | 統一的な位置決め（推奨: 左上共通ボタン） |

**推奨実装パターン**:
- DiffCanvasLeft / DiffCanvasRight の2つコンポーネントに分割
- 各自で独立 ReactFlowProvider を持つ
- 外側（EditorLayout）で onViewportChange を購読し、状態で同期ズーム・パン制御
- DiffTextPanel は両ファイルの統合テキストdiffを表示（変更なし）

## 調査結果：差分比較のSource Code Diffパネル用Mermaid記法生成

差分比較モードで使うMermaid記法の生成方法について、5つの主要関数のシグネチャと処理を報告します。

---

### 1. **generateMermaid** (`src/lib/mermaid/generate.ts`)

**シグネチャ:**
```typescript
export function generateMermaid(
  nodes: FlowNode[],
  edges: FlowEdge[],
  direction: FlowDirection,
  definitions?: ComponentDefinition[]
): string
```

**入力:**
- `nodes: FlowNode[]` — React Flowのノード配列（position, style, dataを含む）
- `edges: FlowEdge[]` — React Flowのエッジ配列
- `direction: FlowDirection` — "TD"または"LR"
- `definitions?: ComponentDefinition[]` — コンポーネント定義（オプション）

**出力:**
- `string` — 標準Mermaid記法（`graph TD` / `graph LR`から始まる完全な文字列）

**処理概要:**
- 通常ノードを`nodeToMermaid()`で変換
- コンポーネントインスタンスを`subgraph`で展開出力（Start/End含む完全な内部構造）
- エッジを`edgeToMermaid()`で変換
- ブリッジエッジ（`edge.data?.isBridgeEdge`）はスキップ
- 内部エッジ（両端が子ノード）もスキップ

**重要:** FlowNodeはReact Flowの実行時オブジェクト（position, styleは最終確定値）で、.flowmaidファイルのレイアウト情報とは別

---

### 2. **serialize** (`src/lib/flowmaid/serialize.ts`)

**シグネチャ:**
```typescript
export function serialize(
  nodes: FlowNode[],
  edges: FlowEdge[],
  direction: FlowDirection,
  componentDefinitions?: ComponentDefinition[],
  options?: SerializeOptions
): string
```

**入力:**
- `nodes: FlowNode[]`, `edges: FlowEdge[]` — React Flowのランタイムデータ
- `direction: FlowDirection`
- `componentDefinitions?: ComponentDefinition[]`
- `options?: SerializeOptions` — `{ includeUnusedDefinitions?: boolean }`

**出力:**
- `string` — 形式: `--- mermaid ---\n{mermaid}\n\n--- layout ---\n{yaml}`

**処理:**
1. `generateMermaid()`でmermaidセクション生成
2. ノード/エッジから`FlowmaidNodeLayout`/`FlowmaidEdgeLayout`を抽出（デフォルト値は省略）
3. `stringify(layout)` (yaml)でyamlセクション生成
4. 2つのセクションを結合

**注:** `.flowmaid`ファイルは**フル状態** = mermaid + layout、どちらも必須

---

### 3. **parseLayoutOnly** (`src/lib/flowmaid/deserialize.ts`)

**シグネチャ:**
```typescript
export function parseLayoutOnly(content: string): FlowmaidLayout
```

**入力:**
- `content: string` — `.flowmaid`ファイルの全文字列

**出力:**
- `FlowmaidLayout` — `{ direction, nodes, edges, componentDefinitions? }`

**処理:**
1. `"--- layout ---"`を探し、その後のYAML部を抽出
2. `parse(yaml)`でYAMLパース
3. 型として`FlowmaidLayout`を返す

**重要:** mermaidセクション(上部)は読まない → 差分比較でレイアウト情報を直接比較するのに最適

---

### 4. **DiffTextPanel** (`src/components/diffComparison/DiffTextPanel.tsx`)

**プロパティ:**
```typescript
interface DiffTextPanelProps {
  diffResult: DiffResult;  // computeDiff()の出力
  filters: DiffFilters;    // { added, deleted, modified }
  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
  onItemClick: (targetId: string, targetType: "node" | "edge" | "componentDef") => void;
}
```

**現在の機能:**
- `formatTextDiff(diffResult, filters, baseLayout, compareLayout)`を呼び出し
- git diffスタイルの行リスト（added/deleted/context/section）を表示
- 行番号 + 差分マーク（+/-）+ テキスト
- `targetId`がある行をクリック可能にして`onItemClick`をトリガー

**現在の処理:**
- YAMLテキストベースの行差分（LCS）
- ノード/エッジのIDをコンテキストスタックで推測
- フィルタで加除/削除行を表示/非表示

---

### 5. **formatTextDiff** (`src/lib/diff/formatTextDiff.ts`)

**シグネチャ:**
```typescript
export function formatTextDiff(
  result: DiffResult,
  filters: DiffFilters,
  baseLayout?: FlowmaidLayout,
  compareLayout?: FlowmaidLayout
): DiffTextLine[]
```

**入力:**
- `result: DiffResult` — computeDiff()の出力（現在は未使用）
- `filters: DiffFilters` — `{ added, deleted, modified }`フラグ
- `baseLayout, compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout` — 2つのレイアウト

**出力:**
- `DiffTextLine[]` 配列
  ```typescript
  interface DiffTextLine {
    type: "added" | "deleted" | "context" | "section";
    text: string;
    lineNumber?: number;
    targetId?: string;
    targetType?: "node" | "edge" | "componentDef";
    diffKind?: DiffKind;
  }
  ```

**処理フロー:**
1. `layoutToYaml(baseLayout)` / `layoutToYaml(compareLayout)` — YAMLに変換
2. `computeLineDiff(baseLines, compareLines)` — LCS風の行差分計算（added/deleted/context）
3. `updateContextStack(line)` — 現在行がYAMLのどのセクション/キーにあるかを追跡
4. `findTargetForLine(text, contextStack)` — ノードID/エッジIDを推測
5. `filters`で加除/削除行をフィルタ

**注意点:**
- `result: DiffResult`パラメータは渡されているが**未使用**
- layoutを直接YAMLテキストに変換して比較（structured diffではない）

---

## 関連する型定義

### FlowmaidLayout
```typescript
interface FlowmaidLayout {
  direction: FlowDirection;                     // "TD" | "LR"
  nodes: Record<string, FlowmaidNodeLayout>;    // ID → ノード情報
  edges: Record<string, FlowmaidEdgeLayout>;    // ID → エッジ情報
  componentDefinitions?: ComponentDefinition[];
}
```

### DiffResult
```typescript
interface DiffResult {
  nodeDiffs: NodeDiff[];           // kind: added|deleted|modified|unchanged
  edgeDiffs: EdgeDiff[];
  componentDefDiffs: ComponentDefDiff[];
}
```

---

## 主要な発見

### Source Code Diffパネルで使うMermaidの生成方法

**現在の流れ:**
1. `baseLayout` → `layoutToYaml()` → base YAMLテキスト
2. `compareLayout` → `layoutToYaml()` → compare YAMLテキスト
3. `computeLineDiff()` でテキスト行の差分を計算
4. 結果を表示（**YAMLテキストベース**）

**Mermaid表示の有無:**
- DiffTextPanelには**mermaid記法を表示する機能はない** → YAMLレイアウト情報のテキスト差分のみ
- Mermaidプレビューを追加するには、`generateMermaid(baseLayout.nodes, baseLayout.edges, ...)`が必要だが、FlowmaidLayoutにはFlowNodeの実行時フィールド（position, style等）がないため直接使えない

**解決方法:**
- `FlowmaidLayout` → `FlowNode/FlowEdge` に変換する中間ステップが必要
- または、`layoutToYaml()`と並行して`layoutToMermaid()`を作成

---
