## Implementation Plan: Side-by-Side Diff Comparison

### 1. Architecture Overview

The current diff comparison uses a single ReactFlow canvas with overlays (DiffOverlay) for a unified/merged view. The new design requires:

- **Upper area**: Two independent ReactFlow canvases side-by-side (Left=Previous/Base, Right=Current/Compare), with synchronized zoom/pan
- **Lower area**: Collapsible Source Code Diff panel with YAML unified diff (existing) and Mermaid side-by-side diff (new)
- **Layout change**: From horizontal split (canvas | text) to vertical split (side-by-side canvases / source code diff panel)

### 2. File Changes

#### 2.1. `src/components/diffComparison/DiffCanvas.tsx` -- Full Rewrite

**Remove:**
- `DiffOverlay` component (SVG dashed outlines + HTML toggle controls)
- `DiffGroup` interface and `buildDiffGroups` function
- `DiffViewMode` toggle (diff/base/compare)
- `groupStates` per-group toggle logic
- Merged node/edge building logic

**Keep and extract to shared utility:**
- `buildNodeFromLayout(id, n, extraStyle?)` -- move to `src/lib/diff/buildDiffNodes.ts`
- `buildPlainNodesEdges(layout)` -- move to `src/lib/diff/buildDiffNodes.ts`
- `DIFF_COLORS` constant

**New structure:**

```
DiffCanvas (outer wrapper)
  ├── DiffSideBySideCanvases (upper area)
  │   ├── DiffSingleCanvas (left = base, with ReactFlowProvider)
  │   └── DiffSingleCanvas (right = compare, with ReactFlowProvider)
  └── (no text panel here -- moved to EditorLayout level)
```

**New props for `DiffCanvas`:**
```typescript
interface DiffCanvasProps {
  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
  diffResult: DiffResult;
  filters: DiffFilters;
  onExit: () => void;
  baseFileName: string;
  compareFileName: string;
}
```

**New inner component `DiffSingleCanvas`:**
```typescript
interface DiffSingleCanvasProps {
  layout: FlowmaidLayout;
  diffResult: DiffResult;
  filters: DiffFilters;
  side: "base" | "compare";
  fileName: string;
  /** Shared viewport state for sync */
  syncViewport: Viewport | null;
  onViewportChange: (viewport: Viewport) => void;
}
```

**Badge rendering approach:**
- After building nodes with `buildPlainNodesEdges`, annotate each node with its `DiffKind` using `diffResult.nodeDiffs`
- Use a wrapper node type or post-process the node style to add a CSS class
- Render badges as absolute-positioned HTML elements within ReactFlow's `<Panel>` or as a custom node wrapper layer
- Better approach: Use the `nodeTypes` from the main app as-is, but add a `DiffBadgeOverlay` component (similar to old `DiffOverlay`) that reads viewport and renders HTML badges at node positions
- Badge styling: small rounded rectangle with glow effect (`box-shadow: 0 0 8px <color>`)

**Viewport synchronization pattern:**
```typescript
// In DiffCanvas parent:
const [sharedViewport, setSharedViewport] = useState<Viewport | null>(null);
const syncSourceRef = useRef<"left" | "right" | null>(null);

// Each DiffSingleCanvas:
// - onViewportChange: if this canvas is the source, update sharedViewport
// - useEffect on syncViewport: if this canvas is NOT the source, call setViewport()
// - Use onMoveStart/onMoveEnd to track which canvas is the interaction source
```

Loop prevention: Use a `useRef` flag (`isSyncing`) that is set to `true` when programmatically calling `setViewport()`, and skip `onViewportChange` callbacks when this flag is true.

**Node filtering per side:**
- Base canvas: Show only nodes from `baseLayout`; deleted nodes shown normally, added nodes hidden
- Compare canvas: Show only nodes from `compareLayout`; added nodes shown normally, deleted nodes hidden
- Modified nodes: Show the respective version on each side
- Unchanged nodes: Show on both sides

**Badge logic:**
- ADDED badge (green glow): Only on compare (right) canvas
- DELETED badge (red glow): Only on base (left) canvas
- MODIFIED badge (yellow glow): On both canvases

#### 2.2. New file: `src/lib/diff/buildDiffNodes.ts`

Extract shared utilities from current `DiffCanvas.tsx`:
- `buildNodeFromLayout(id, n, extraStyle?)` -- FlowmaidNodeLayout to FlowNode
- `buildPlainNodesEdges(layout)` -- FlowmaidLayout to {FlowNode[], FlowEdge[]}
- `DIFF_COLORS` constant
- New: `buildSideNodes(layout, diffResult, filters, side: "base" | "compare")` -- Build nodes for one side with appropriate filtering

#### 2.3. New file: `src/components/diffComparison/DiffBadgeOverlay.tsx`

A component that renders floating badges at node positions, similar to the old `DiffOverlay` but simpler (no grouping, no toggle).

```typescript
interface DiffBadgeOverlayProps {
  nodes: FlowNode[];
  diffResult: DiffResult;
  filters: DiffFilters;
  side: "base" | "compare";
}
```

- Uses `useViewport()` to convert flow-space to screen-space
- Renders small badges (e.g., 60x20px) at the top-right corner of each changed node
- Badge text: "ADDED" / "DELETED" / "MODIFIED" (from i18n)
- Badge styling: rounded-full, font-size 9px, with `box-shadow: 0 0 6px 2px <color>` for glow effect

#### 2.4. `src/components/diffComparison/DiffTextPanel.tsx` -- Major Refactor

**New features:**
1. **Collapsible toggle**: Panel can be collapsed/expanded (handled by parent via ResizablePanelGroup)
2. **Mode switch**: YAML unified diff (existing) vs Mermaid side-by-side diff (new)
3. **Mermaid side-by-side**: Left and right columns showing Mermaid output for base and compare

**New props:**
```typescript
interface DiffTextPanelProps {
  diffResult: DiffResult;
  filters: DiffFilters;
  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;
  onItemClick: (targetId: string, targetType: "node" | "edge" | "componentDef") => void;
  // New: for Mermaid generation
  baseFileName: string;
  compareFileName: string;
}
```

**New internal state:**
```typescript
type DiffTextMode = "yaml" | "mermaid";
const [mode, setMode] = useState<DiffTextMode>("yaml");
```

**Mermaid side-by-side diff implementation:**
- Use `buildPlainNodesEdges(baseLayout)` and `buildPlainNodesEdges(compareLayout)` to get FlowNode[]/FlowEdge[]
- Call `generateMermaid(nodes, edges, layout.direction, layout.componentDefinitions)` for each side
- Split into lines, compute LCS diff (reuse `computeLineDiff` from `formatTextDiff.ts`)
- Render two columns side-by-side with line numbers and highlights

**Extract `computeLineDiff` from `formatTextDiff.ts`:**
- Move `computeLineDiff` to a separate export so both YAML and Mermaid diff can use it

#### 2.5. `src/components/layout/EditorLayout.tsx` -- Layout Change for Diff Compare Step

**Current** (diff compare step):
```
ResizablePanelGroup(horizontal)
  ├── ResizablePanel: DiffCanvas
  └── ResizablePanel: DiffTextPanel
```

**New** (diff compare step):
```
ResizablePanelGroup(vertical)
  ├── ResizablePanel: DiffCanvas (contains side-by-side canvases internally)
  └── ResizablePanel: DiffTextPanel (collapsible, with YAML/Mermaid toggle)
```

Changes:
- Switch `orientation` from `"horizontal"` to `"vertical"` for the diff compare view
- Add collapse/expand toggle for the bottom panel (similar to `ToggleResizeHandle` pattern)
- Adjust default sizes: upper canvas ~65%, lower text ~35%
- Remove `flashTarget` state and related flash logic (no longer needed with badge approach)

**New constants:**
```typescript
const DIFF_UPPER_DEFAULT_SIZE = 65;
const DIFF_UPPER_MIN_SIZE = 30;
const DIFF_LOWER_DEFAULT_SIZE = 35;
const DIFF_LOWER_MIN_SIZE = 10;
```

#### 2.6. `src/components/diffComparison/index.ts` -- Update exports

Add export for any new component (e.g., `DiffBadgeOverlay` if exported separately).

#### 2.7. `src/lib/i18n/locales.ts` -- New translation keys

New keys needed:
- `diffSourceCode`: "Source Code" / "ソースコード"
- `diffYamlDiff`: "YAML Diff" / "YAML差分"
- `diffMermaidDiff`: "Mermaid Diff" / "Mermaid差分"
- `diffPrevious`: "Previous" / "変更前"
- `diffCurrent`: "Current" / "変更後"

Existing keys to keep: `diffKindAdded`, `diffKindDeleted`, `diffKindModified`, `diffBack`, etc.

Keys to remove or repurpose: `diffViewDiff`, `diffViewBase`, `diffViewCompare` (the old Diff/Base/Compare toggle is removed).

### 3. Implementation Steps (Sequencing)

**Step 1: Extract shared utilities**
- Create `src/lib/diff/buildDiffNodes.ts` with `buildNodeFromLayout`, `buildPlainNodesEdges`, `DIFF_COLORS`
- Export `computeLineDiff` from `src/lib/diff/formatTextDiff.ts`
- Update imports in existing `DiffCanvas.tsx` to use the extracted module

**Step 2: Create DiffBadgeOverlay**
- New file `src/components/diffComparison/DiffBadgeOverlay.tsx`
- Badge rendering with glow effect at node top-right corner
- Uses `useViewport()` for coordinate conversion

**Step 3: Rewrite DiffCanvas**
- Replace single ReactFlow with two `DiffSingleCanvas` components
- Each wraps its own `ReactFlowProvider`
- Implement viewport sync with `useRef` loop prevention
- File name labels above each canvas
- Integrate `DiffBadgeOverlay` into each `DiffSingleCanvas`

**Step 4: Refactor DiffTextPanel**
- Add YAML/Mermaid mode toggle (tab/button group at the top)
- Add Mermaid side-by-side diff renderer
- Move `computeLineDiff` to a shared location

**Step 5: Update EditorLayout**
- Change diff compare layout from horizontal to vertical split
- Add collapse/expand for the bottom Source Code panel
- Remove flash-related state (`diffFlashTarget`, `diffFlashSeqRef`)
- Wire new props to updated components

**Step 6: Update i18n**
- Add new translation keys to both `en` and `ja` locales

**Step 7: Update exports and cleanup**
- Update `src/components/diffComparison/index.ts`
- Remove dead code from old overlay approach

### 4. Key Design Decisions and Trade-offs

**Viewport sync approach**: Using `onMoveStart`/`onMoveEnd` to track the interaction source canvas, plus a `useRef` flag to prevent infinite sync loops. This is more robust than debouncing because it handles continuous drag without lag.

**Badge rendering**: Using viewport-transformed HTML overlays (same approach as old `DiffOverlay`) rather than custom node types. This avoids modifying the node rendering pipeline and keeps badges as a pure visual layer.

**Mermaid generation for diff**: Requires converting `FlowmaidLayout` back to `FlowNode[]`/`FlowEdge[]` via `buildPlainNodesEdges`, then calling `generateMermaid`. Component definitions are available on the layout object (`layout.componentDefinitions`).

**Performance**: Two ReactFlow instances will approximately double the rendering cost. Since both canvases are read-only (`nodesDraggable={false}`, `nodesConnectable={false}`), the overhead is mainly initial render and zoom/pan. This should be acceptable for the diff comparison use case where interactivity is minimal.

**No grouping in new design**: The old design grouped nearby changed nodes with dashed rectangles. The new badge approach is per-node, which is simpler and more aligned with the side-by-side concept where each node's status is independently visible.

### 5. Potential Challenges

1. **Viewport sync jitter**: If both canvases fire `onViewportChange` rapidly, there could be visual jitter. Mitigate with `requestAnimationFrame` batching and the `isSyncing` ref guard.

2. **Initial fitView coordination**: Both canvases will have `fitView` enabled, but they may compute different initial viewports if base and compare have different node positions. Solution: compute a shared bounding box that encompasses both layouts, and set the initial viewport to that.

3. **Mermaid diff accuracy**: The LCS-based diff in `computeLineDiff` may produce suboptimal alignment for large changes. This is acceptable for an initial implementation and can be improved later with a proper diff library.

4. **ResizablePanel vertical orientation**: The existing `ResizablePanelGroup` supports `orientation="vertical"` via `aria-[orientation=vertical]:flex-col`. This should work out of the box, but the collapse handle needs to be horizontal (chevron up/down instead of left/right).

### Critical Files for Implementation
- `/root/flowmaid/src/components/diffComparison/DiffCanvas.tsx` - Full rewrite: replace unified view with two side-by-side ReactFlow instances plus viewport sync
- `/root/flowmaid/src/components/diffComparison/DiffTextPanel.tsx` - Major refactor: add YAML/Mermaid toggle, Mermaid side-by-side diff renderer
- `/root/flowmaid/src/components/layout/EditorLayout.tsx` - Layout change: horizontal to vertical split, collapsible bottom panel, remove flash state
- `/root/flowmaid/src/lib/diff/formatTextDiff.ts` - Extract `computeLineDiff` for reuse by Mermaid diff
- `/root/flowmaid/src/lib/i18n/locales.ts` - Add new translation keys for side-by-side UI labels

各側でそれぞれの版を表示\n\nProps:\n```typescript\ninterface DiffCanvasProps {\n  baseLayout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  compareLayout: FlowmaidLayout;\n  diffResult: DiffResult;\n  filters: DiffFilters;\n  onExit: () =>
